Unlike containment-based approaches that risk outpacing growing model capabilities, escalation channels redirect capability toward disclosure rather than exploitation.

</details>


### [128] [Call Neighbours Yourself: Graph Walks with Destination-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29588)
*Yilun Liu,Boyu Luo,Yanran Tang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CNY通过允许LLM基于图游走动态展开邻居并采用目的地条件自蒸馏进行延迟回报训练，从而在TAG推理上取得更好、更具迁移性的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成前固定可访问邻居集合，导致推理只能在静态上下文上进行，无法在推理时获取缺失证据；因此主张将邻居选择作为推理的一部分，让模型在需要时主动探查图结构以获得额外证据。

Method: 提出Call Neighbours Yourself (CNY)框架：通过受拓扑约束的图游走动作让模型主动探索邻居；为候选邻居提供轻量预览并学习何时展开更多邻居；引入目的地条件的on-policy自蒸馏（destination-conditioned on-policy self-distillation）来解决邻居探索的延迟回报问题，通过在揭示邻居内容后回溯评估并将动作偏好变化转化为动作级训练信号。

Result: 在统一的原始文本设置下，于标准TAG推理基准上实验表明：CNY稳健优于固定上下文的后训练基线；所学探索策略能迁移到未见过的图以及训练阶段未遇到的图级任务。

Conclusion: CNY使LLM能在推理过程中动态选择并展开图邻居，克服了静态上下文限制，从而提升了在文本属性图（TAG）上的推理性能，并学得可迁移的探索策略。

Abstract: Reasoning over text-attributed graphs (TAGs) requires large language models (LLMs) to combine a node's text with evidence distributed across its neighbourhood. Existing methods fix the set of accessible neighbours before generation, forcing reasoning to operate over a static context and preventing the model from acquiring missing evidence during inference. We argue that neighbour selection should itself be part of the reasoning process. To this end, we propose Call Neighbours Yourself (CNY), a framework that enables LLMs to proactively explore graph neighbourhoods through topology-constrained graph-walk actions. Instead of reasoning over a pre-selected neighbour set, CNY exposes lightweight neighbour previews and learns when to expand candidate neighbours for additional evidence. To address the delayed-credit challenge of neighbour exploration, we introduce destination-conditioned on-policy self-distillation, which retrospectively evaluates a selected neighbour after its content is revealed and converts the resulting change in action preference into an action-level training signal. Experiments on standard TAG reasoning benchmarks under a unified raw-text setting show that CNY consistently outperforms fixed-context post-training baselines. Furthermore, the learned exploration policy transfers to unseen graphs and to a graph-level task not encountered during training. Code is available at https://github.com/superallen13/CNY.

</details>


### [129] [Not Safe for All: Auditing the Dialect Penalty in Text-to-Image Safety Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29589)
*Minkyu Kim,Juhwan Choi,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T2I系统的文本级安全过滤对非标准方言表现出系统性偏差（“方言惩罚”），问题可归因于训练数据不平衡且可通过组平衡重训练缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现有T2I安全管控是否公平地对待不同母语或方言使用者，识别并修复由于文本处理阶段导致的群体不平等。

Method: 对23,080对提示词、5种英语方言进行评估；比较多种文本级与像素级护栏（NSFW-T、LatentGuard、OpenAI Moderation API）；进行受控错字消融；追溯训练数据不均衡并尝试组平衡重训练与GroupDRO消融实验。

Result: 发现文本级过滤器在方向相反地对方言过度或不足触发（偏差差距可达+28.29个百分点）；像素级生成器对方言影响甚微；惩罚主要在文本处理阶段产生并传导到后续护栏；通过组平衡重训练可显著缓解偏差，效果归因于均衡暴露而非GroupDRO的worst-group目标。

Conclusion: 文本到图像（T2I）安全护栏在面对非标准英语方言时存在系统性不公平，称为“方言惩罚”，该惩罚源自文本过滤器对表面语言特征的触发而非语义意图。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) safety guardrails fail to generalize equitably to non-standard dialects. Evaluating 23,080 paired prompts across five English dialects, we formalize this failure as the dialect penalty, where filters trigger based on linguistic surface features rather than semantic intent. Text-level filters fail in opposing directions: NSFW-T over-flags benign dialect prompts and LatentGuard over-flags toxic ones (bias gaps up to +28.29 pp), while the OpenAI Moderation API under-detects them. A controlled typo ablation confirms this penalty originates from flagging dialectal features, not generic out-of-distribution sensitivity. The pixel-level generator is largely dialect-agnostic; the penalty enters at text processing and cascades unevenly to post-hoc guardrails. We show this bias tracks training data imbalance and is mitigable via group-balanced retraining, with an ablation attributing the gain to balanced exposure rather than to the worst-group objective of GroupDRO (group distributionally robust optimization). Current pipelines systematically fail dialect speakers, an equity failure masked by mean accuracy benchmarks. Our official code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/minguinho26/dialect-penalty-t2i.
  Content Warning: This paper contains offensive, toxic, or disturbing text prompts and generated images.

</details>


### [130] [Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Skills: Architecture, Lifecycle, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29596)
*Sanket Badhe,Deep Shah,Priyanka Tiwari,Nehal Kathrotia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统化提出并规范化“agentic skills”范式，通过九阶段生命周期与参考架构把执行性技能作为连接规划与确定性执行的关键构件，讨论实现、评估与安全治理问题，确立研究与工程路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型代理在复杂长程任务中受限于可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性；传统基于大提示或无状态工具调用的方法难以扩展，因而需要模块化、可重用且可执行的程序化抽象来承载执行知识。

Method: 对现有工作进行系统化整理与概念化，形式化技能定义，划分并论述九阶段生命周期（发现、创作/表示、存储、检索/路由、组合/编排、执行/修复、终身适应、经验评估、安全治理），并对市场、注册表、对抗威胁、运行时验证与防御机制进行分析，辅以跨领域实现的分类讨论。

Result: 给出一个系统化的架构与生命周期模型，明确技能的语义与工程构件，讨论实现要点与安全/治理问题，列举若干领域的实现范式，并指出持续学习与基准现实性等关键开放问题，为后续研究与工程实践提供路线图与研究议程。

Conclusion: 本文确立了“agentic skills”作为构建可扩展、稳健且可验证自治语言代理的基础范式，提出并形式化了技能（skill）为连接高层认知规划与确定性执行环境的外化程序化知识，并给出统一的参考架构与生命周期框架。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face reliability, context consumption, and execution stability bottlenecks when deployed on complex, long-horizon tasks. While monolithic prompt engineering and stateless tool-calling paradigms struggle to scale, the field is rapidly converging toward \emph{agentic skills}: modular procedural abstractions that externalize execution knowledge into reusable, executable, and portable artifacts. This paper establishes a unified systems foundation and reference architecture for the agentic skills ecosystem. We formalize skills as externalized procedural knowledge bridging high-level cognitive planning with deterministic execution environments, and systematically delineate the architecture across a nine-stage lifecycle: autonomous discovery, authoring and representation formats, memory storage, dynamic retrieval and routing, composition and orchestration, execution and repair, lifelong adaptation, empirical evaluation, and security governance. We further examine marketplace dynamics, public registries, and emerging adversarial threat vectors, alongside runtime verification and defense mechanisms. Finally, we categorize system implementations across software engineering, operating system navigation, embodied robotics, and scientific discovery, while highlighting critical open challenges in continual learning and benchmark realism. This work establishes agentic skills as a foundational paradigm for building scalable, robust, and verifiable autonomous language agents.

</details>


### [131] [LLMs Interpret, Embeddings Organize, Graphs Emerge: Agent-Driven Compilation of Scientific Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29612)
*Shi-Ju Ran,Kun Zhang,Xi Wu,Liu-Si Yang,Wen-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现一种将论文持续编译进可检查的知识图谱与Wiki的系统（ASKS），并通过对56篇论文的运行展示其在保持源可追溯性、低层级变动和添加性增长方面的行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立并维持一个可追溯、可被多任务利用的科学知识底层，使解释能跨任务迁移并保持到源证据的路径。通过将原始文献编译为可读的Wiki视图和机器可处理的语义表示，实现长期的知识积累与审查。

Method: 对每篇来源用大模型生成可读Wiki视图与机器语义；用确定性检查把语义转为GraphDelta；使用向量嵌入几何和图规则来整合GraphDelta进全局持久图；每次引入都记录为可检视的状态转移并链接回源文件。

Result: 实现了ASKS系统：对每个来源生成可读Wiki与机器语义，使用确定性检查转为文档局部的GraphDelta，结合嵌入几何与显式图规则将更改整合进持久状态。对56篇同一研究项目论文的编译展示了分支存活、跨论文支持、谱系、覆盖率与变动等可测量指标，保留了到来源记录的可追溯链接。

Conclusion: ASKS能把文献转成可审计的状态变迁，维持源记录链接并支持对研究者作品的时序性画像。系统展示高层组织稳定、节点主要是添加性增长，并为量子-张量网络研究路线提供明确的谱系与覆盖分析。

Abstract: Sustained scientific work requires a knowledge substrate that carries interpretation across tasks and preserves paths to source evidence. We call this process \emph{scientific knowledge compilation} and implement it in ASKS, the \emph{Agent-Driven Scientific Knowledge System}. For each source, an LLM produces a readable Wiki view and machine-facing semantics. Deterministic checks convert the latter into a document-local GraphDelta, and embedding geometry together with explicit graph rules integrates the proposed changes into persistent state. Each ingest is an inspectable state transition over accumulated knowledge, with compiled Wiki and graph views linked to the preserved source record. We examine this process by chronologically compiling 56 published papers from one research program. Branch survival, cross-paper support, lineage, coverage, and churn yield a source-traceable author research portrait centered on tensor-network methods, with branches into quantum many-body research, tensor-network machine learning, and quantum-AI-oriented directions. In this run, higher-level Hub organization remains stable and low-churn. Canonical-node growth is predominantly additive. Graph-level measurements and navigation paths retain links to the source records from which they were compiled.

</details>


### [132] [Detect Before You Attribute: Cascade Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29646)
*Jiayi Zhang,Zexin Wang,Degang Sun,Changhua Pei,Fei Sun,Gaogang Xie,Jingjing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DUOTRACE detects anomalous agent executions via a VAE with semantic-structural representations and supplies focused trajectory evidence to LLM-based attribution, yielding notable accuracy gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based agents suffer execution failures and existing attribution methods either miss fine-grained semantics or degrade over long trajectories; accurate, focused failure attribution is needed to improve reliability.

Method: A detect-before-attribute pipeline using VAE-based anomaly detection on agent trajectories with dual-view semantic-structural node representations, Tree-LSTM trajectory encoder, and prefix-chain plus LLM-based data augmentation; acts as a plug-and-play filter for existing LLM-based attribution methods.

Result: Across six LLM-based attribution baselines, DUOTRACE improves agent-level attribution accuracy by 8.7% and step-level attribution accuracy by 7.0%.

Conclusion: DUOTRACE effectively improves failure attribution for LLM-based agents by detecting anomalous executions before performing attribution, providing focused evidence to downstream models.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown strong potential in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning, yet they remain vulnerable to execution failures. Accurate failure attribution is therefore critical for improving agent reliability. Existing topology- and spectrum-based methods exploit trajectory structures but often overlook fine-grained semantics, while LLM-based attribution methods capture semantic cues but suffer from long-context degradation over lengthy trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DUOTRACE, a plug-and-play detection filter for LLM-based failure attribution. DUOTRACE follows a detect-before-attribute paradigm: it first detects anomalous executions and then supplies focused trajectory evidence to downstream LLM-based attribution methods. For effective VAE-based anomaly detection on agent trajectories, DUOTRACE integrates dual-view semantic-structural node representations, a Tree-LSTM-based trajectory encoder, and prefix-chain- and LLM-based data augmentation to handle heterogeneous nodes, hierarchical execution structures, and limited failure data. Experiments with six LLM-based attribution baselines show that DUOTRACE improves agent-level and step-level attribution accuracy by 8.7% and 7.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [133] [Ideation Arena: Evaluating LLM Generated Research Ideas with Battle-style Human Expert Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29696)
*Zhiyu Chen,Keyu Zhao,Jigao Fu,Dong Liang,Yanbiao Wu,Jiaoyang Li,Haidong Xue,Xinhua Zeng,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了基于专家两两对比与Elo评分的Ideation Arena，用于在共享文献语境下评价LLM与研究代理的科研构想；实验证明代理间效果差异显著，且自动评审器尚不能完全替代人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研构想的价值难以用客观唯一标准衡量，缺乏参考答案；需要一种可比较、可规模化且更接近人类专家偏好的评估方法来衡量LLM与研究代理在科研生成中的实际效用。

Method: 构建共享闭域文献上下文，向14个前沿LLM与5种基于两种基础模型的研究代理提供相同起点；收集105名计算机科学研究者的6000余次双盲两两比较；用Elo评级生成排行榜；通过评审者一致性与稳健性分析验证排名稳定性；构建Ideation Arena Eval以检验自动评估器（LLM评审）与人类偏好的对齐度。

Result: 在共享上下文协议下得到了可复现且稳健的Elo排行榜；发现部分代理确实能在构想质量上超过其骨干模型，而部分代理无益甚至退化；当前LLM作为评审者表现有限，最佳者在Overall Quality上仅达72.56% Soft Accuracy；代码、数据与排行榜已开源。

Conclusion: 本文提出了通过双盲两两比较的“战斗式”平台（Ideation Arena）来评估由大模型与研究型代理生成的科研构想，并在共享的文献语境下构建了基于Elo分的优先级排行榜，结果表明不同代理在构想质量上差异显著，且当前自动评估器尚不能可靠地复制专家偏好。

Abstract: Evaluating research ideas generated by LLMs is difficult because their scientific value cannot be fully determined by objective criteria, and no single reference answer specifies what counts as a good idea. To address this challenge, we introduce Ideation Arena, a battle style platform that evaluates research ideas through pairwise human assessment. Ideation Arena evaluates ideas generated by 14 frontier LLMs and 5 research agent architectures built on 2 base models. To ensure a common starting point, Ideation Arena builds shared literature contexts from papers familiar to the participating researchers and provides the same contexts to all LLMs and agents. We collect over 6,000 double blind pairwise comparisons from 105 active computer science researchers and construct an Elo rating leaderboard of proposal-stage expert preferences in computer science under a shared closed-context protocol. We validate the rankings through interrater agreement and robustness analyses, showing that the leaderboard remains stable under changes in annotator composition and domain coverage. Our results show substantial variation in agent effectiveness, with some frameworks improving ideation quality over their backbones and others offering little benefit or even underperforming their base models. We further construct Ideation Arena Eval, a benchmark for assessing whether automated evaluators align with human preferences in research ideation. Experiments with current LLM judges show that they still cannot reliably reproduce expert preferences, with the best judge reaching 72.56% Soft Accuracy on Overall Quality. Our code, data, and leaderboards are available at https://github.com/foss12138/Research-Ideation-Arena.

</details>


### [134] [PAGE-RAG: Provenance-Aware Graph Evidence Promotion for Fixed-Budget Multi-hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29753)
*Haokun Deng,Xunkai Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在检索候选上构建查询局部图并用溯源感知评分最小化提升支持事实，PAGE-RAG 有效弥合连通性与支持性的差距，从而在多跳RAG任务上显著提升支持与答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多跳QA中，窄检索可能漏掉必要跳跃，扩展检索则引入话题干扰，亟需一种查询感知的选择层利用关系结构在生成前过滤候选；这种问题与知识库格式无关，适用于多种检索来源。

Method: 对检索到的候选条目构建查询局部图、记录条目间的连接原因（将连接视为支持假设而非直接支持），对候选路径基于相关性、来源追踪元数据、特异性、枢纽性、噪声和连贯性等信号进行评分，并采用最小充分选择策略将被评分为支持的事实提升到紧凑的reader上下文；该提升阶段可作为完整流水线或插入现有RAG系统的插件。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上、在相同最终上下文预算下，PAGE-RAG 在以强检索器为基线的加权平均上将支持事实F1提升10.4点、答案F1提升3.3点；作为插件亦能改善各种RAG后端（面向推理、压缩、图检索、文档/块级系统）。

Conclusion: PAGE-RAG 提出并验证了一种在检索增强生成（RAG）流水线中基于查询构建临时图并进行溯源感知证据提升的方法，通过选择性地将支持性事实提升到阅读器上下文来桥接检索连通性与事实支持之间的缺口，从而提升多跳问答的支持事实识别与最终答案质量。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering in retrieval-augmented gener?ation (RAG) often benefits from retrieving beyond the few candidates that will finally be read: narrow retrieval can miss an indispensable hop, while expanded retrieval introduces topical distractors. This challenge is not tied to a particu?lar knowledge-base format. Candidate pools may come from standalone retrievers, standard RAG backends, or graph-based retrieval pipelines. What is needed is a query-aware selection layer that can use relational structure to filter candidates be?fore generation. PAGE-RAG addresses this setting by using a graph as a temporary selection structure, rather than assum?ing a graph-structured knowledge base. It builds a query-local graph over retrieved candidates, records why candidates are connected, and treats each connection as a support hypothe?sis rather than support itself. We identify the resulting failure mode as a connectivity-support gap: connected candidates do not necessarily support the answer. We propose PAGE-RAG, a Provenance-Aware Graph Evidence promotion method that scores candidate paths with relevance, source-tracing meta?data, specificity, hubness, noise, and coherence signals, and applies minimal sufficient selection to promote supporting facts into a compact reader context. PAGE-RAG can serve as a complete retrieval-to-reading pipeline, and the same promo?tion stage can be inserted after existing retrieval or RAG sys?tems without replacing their upstream retrieval logic. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks under the same final bud?get, PAGE-RAG improves support F1 and answer F1 by 10.4 and 3.3 points on a weighted average over a strong retriever. As a plug-in, PAGE-RAG further improves all reported RAG backends, including reasoning-oriented, compression-based, graph-based, and document/chunk-level systems.

</details>


### [135] [FRAMEWORKERS: A Dynamic Multi-Agent Framework for AI-Generated Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29814)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Letian Shi,Deheng Zhang,Ruibo Ming,Mengshun Hu,Dannong Xu,Jian Wang,Danda Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的任务/工作空间驱动多代理框架，通过SFT+GRPO微调Director实现鲁棒、模块化的视频制作编排，实验显示优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化系统管线僵化，通用LLM在长期编排和多模态资产路由上不可靠，视频制作需要长期、多步、资产依赖和动态编排能力。

Method: 中心化Director负责动态任务管理与调用，Assistant在共享Workspace中执行任务并持久化产物。子代理以描述符注册，实现模块化能力。训练上先进行监督微调，再用Group Relative Policy Optimization对描述符条件的任务路由进行强化优化。

Result: 在路由准确率、从运行时失败恢复能力、对未见子代理的泛化性、端到端视频质量和任务覆盖范围上均优于固定管线、单代理和先前多代理方法。

Conclusion: FRAMEWORKERS提出了任务中心、工作空间接地的多代理框架，通过Director不断编辑任务栈并调用Assistant和模块化子代理，实现灵活可扩展的视频制作编排。该方法通过SFT+GRPO微调Director改善了路由可靠性，在实验中优于强LLM规划器和已有方法，具有更高的视频质量与任务覆盖能力。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at synthesizing individual clips, but complete video production requires coordinating a long sequence of interdependent creative steps, including scripting, storyboarding, generation, and editing. It further demands persistent asset management and dynamic task orchestration as intermediate outputs, dependencies, and execution states evolve over time. Existing automated systems typically rely on rigid pipelines that are difficult to adapt to diverse inputs and changing workflows, while general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for long-horizon orchestration and multimodal asset routing. We introduce FRAMEWORKERS, a task-centric and workspace-grounded multi-agent framework for open-ended video production. A central Director formulates video creation as dynamic task management, continuously editing a Task Stack to determine which subtask to execute next and which sub-agent to invoke. An Assistant serves as the execution layer, grounding each selected task in a shared Workspace, retrieving the required assets and context, invoking the assigned sub-agent, and persisting the resulting artifacts. Execution capabilities are exposed through modular sub-agents with registered descriptors, allowing new sub-agents to be integrated without redesigning the orchestration workflow. To improve orchestration reliability, we fine-tune the Director via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for descriptor-conditioned task routing. Experiments show that FRAMEWORKERS outperforms strong LLM planners in routing accuracy, recovers reliably from runtime failures, generalizes to unseen sub-agents without retraining, and achieves higher end-to-end video quality and broader task coverage than fixed pipelines, single-agent systems, and prior multi-agent approaches.

</details>


### [136] [Perceive to Hypothesize, Verify to Ground: An Agentic Reasoning Framework for Open-World Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29880)
*Yutian Jiang,Ruijie Li,Sisuo Lyu,Xixuan Hao,Qingxiang Liu,Yongzi Yu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将地理定位问题改写为“感知-验证”循环，提出GeoPAVE双层智能体框架并发布包含多轮推理轨迹的PAVED数据集，以提高多模态推理的证据可追溯性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing vision-language models for geo-localization hallucinate and drift because they lack explicit evidence-grounded verification. The work aims to mimic human perceive-then-verify reasoning to improve reliability and traceability.

Method: Bi-level agentic framework: (1) perception-based hypothesis generation via single-pass rollouts to produce candidate locations and reasoning trajectories; (2) verification-based evidence grounding using multi-round tool invocations and explicit actions (support, refute, refine) to validate hypotheses.

Result: Proposes GeoPAVE and releases PAVED dataset with multi-hop queries and structured perception-verification traces; claims improved reasoning robustness and reduced perceptual hallucination, with code and data publicly available.

Conclusion: GeoPAVE reframes geo-localization as perceive-then-verify multi-step reasoning, reducing hallucination through explicit evidence grounding and decision actions (support/refute/refine). The introduced PAVED dataset enables rigorous evaluation with structured reasoning traces.

Abstract: Open-world geo-localization requires models to reason over ambiguous visual cues through multi-step reasoning and external knowledge grounding. While recent large vision-language models exhibit strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, existing approaches still suffer from perceptual hallucination and context drift due to the lack of explicit evidence-grounded verification. In this work, we reformulate geo-localization as a human-like perceive-then-verify reasoning problem and propose GeoPAVE (Geo-localization Perception-and-Verification-Engine), a bi-level agentic framework that contains perception-based hypothesis generation via single-pass rollouts and verification-based evidence grounding for decision actions: support, refute, and refine. To support rigorous evaluation, we further introduce PAVED, a novel dataset derived from real-world user check-in data, equipped with comprehensive reasoning trajectories featuring multi-hop queries, multi-round tool invocations, and structured perception-verification traces. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Arandinglv/GeoPAVE.

</details>


### [137] [On the Instance Hardness as a Decision Criterion in TinyML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29913)
*Tobiasz Puslecki,Krzysztof Walkowiak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将tree depth prune instance hardness方法应用于TinyML，通过阈值控制在准确率与能耗之间做出可控权衡；提供了概念验证的初步结果，后续需补充更充分的实验与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型和系统规模增长，推理能耗与计算成本增加。在受内存与计算受限的嵌入式设备（TinyML）上，需要方法在尽量保持性能的同时降低能耗并允许按需求调整精度/能耗平衡。

Method: 在TinyML端引入tree depth prune的instance hardness度量：通过计算样本在决策树/树集中的深度（或剪枝判据）判断样本难度，并设置阈值决定是否进行完整分类，从而减少计算步骤并降低能耗。以概念验证实验测量能耗与分类质量随阈值变化的关系。

Result: 初步实验表明：通过阈值控制可以显著改变单次/总体能耗，同时分类性能仅发生有限下降，证明该方法有潜力作为TinyML能耗-准确率调节手段，但仍属工作进展，需要更全面评估与对比基线。

Conclusion: 初步证明将基于树深度的prune instance hardness方法用于TinyML场景是可行的：通过阈值控制可以在仅受限的分类质量下降下降低推理能耗，从而在准确率与能耗之间实现可控权衡。

Abstract: TinyML includes the implementation of machine learning on devices with limited memory and computing resources. With the development of technology, AI systems continue to scale in terms of size and computational requirements. This forces researchers to adapt methods to be environmentally sustainable by designing techniques for reducing computational costs and energy consumption in inferring AI models, even in small devices. In this work, we present preliminary findings on a novel application of the tree depth prune instance hardness method to the TinyML system. The results indicate that threshold control can change energy consumption with limited classification quality changes. This method allows us to adjust classification accuracy, thereby influencing computational complexity and energy consumption for inference. We present a work in progress with initial results as a proof of concept.

</details>


### [138] [AcrossWAM1.0:A Modular Latent World-Action Stack for Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29937)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcrossWAM1.0不是提出新的潜在子目标算法，而是把LaWAM的潜在世界—动作体系拆成可审计的模块、分离训练/推理图并导出紧凑、位一致的部署检查点；在LIBERO上小骨干替换后性能基本保留，但闭环跨族迁移与更广泛评估尚未完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LaWAM把世界模型、多模态骨干和部署检查点耦合在一起，影响可替换性、可审计性和紧凑部署的可达性。目标是明确模块边界，支持骨干更换、减小推理检查点并提供可验证的导出流程。

Method: 将整体体系拆分为清晰模块：策略适配器产生latent-action与action-generation上下文；保留的潜在世界解码器将预测的过渡锚定到当前场景；流匹配（flow-matching）专家生成连续动作片段；训练时教师与推理图分离并导出可验证的紧凑检查点。用LIBERO数据集（2,000对episode）比较不同视觉骨干替换和跨族适配器烟雾测试。

Result: 在2,000对LIBERO episode上，将Qwen3-VL-2B换为Qwen3.5-0.8B得到97.45%成功率，对比2B的98.00%，差值-0.55个百分点；McNemar检验p=0.266（未显著）；紧凑可推理检查点含1,472.6M独立参数，声称比原2B策略少42.4%，且所有保留张量与源检查点逐位相同。进行了MiniCPM-V适配器的跨族执行烟雾测试，但闭环跨族迁移仍未评估。

Conclusion: AcrossWAM1.0将LaWAM的潜在世界—动作栈模块化并可扩展，提供可审计的部署边界：在保持预测潜在子目标思想的同时，明确划分策略适配器、潜在世界解码器和流匹配动作生成器等模块，实现训练-推理图的分离与可验证导出。实验证明用更小的视觉背骨替换后性能基本保留，且保留张量与源检查点逐位相同。

Abstract: Latent world-action models avoid rendering future pixels by predicting an action-relevant visual subgoal in feature space. LaWAM established this formulation, but its original presentation left the world model, multimodal backbone, and deployment checkpoint tightly coupled. We introduce AcrossWAM1.0, a modularization and scaling study of this latent world-action stack. Rather than presenting latent subgoals as a new algorithm, we make the module boundary explicit: a policy adapter produces latent-action and action-generation contexts; a retained latent world decoder grounds the predicted transition in the current scene;and a flow-matching expert generates continuous action chunks. We further separate training-only teachers from the inference graph and provide a verifiable deployment export. On 2,000 paired LIBERO episodes, replacing a Qwen3-VL-2B backbone with Qwen3.5-0.8B yields 97.45% success versus 98.00% for the 2B model (a-0.55percentage-point difference; exact McNemarp=0.266). This does not prove equivalence, but it meets a prespecified two-point retention criterion. The compact, inference-reachable checkpoint contains 1,472.6M unique parameters, 42.4% fewer than the original 2B policy, while all retained tensors are bitwise identical to the source checkpoint. Cross-family execution is additionally checked with a MiniCPM-V adapter smoke test; closed-loop cross-family transfer remains an open evaluation. AcrossWAM1.0 therefore contributes an auditable software and evaluation boundary for compact latent world-action policies, distinct from LaWAM's original latent-subgoal contribution.

</details>


### [139] [Spatial Matryoshka Training for Multi-Granularity Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29951)
*Trishan Singha Roy,Arkadeep Acharya,Vishwajeet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Nested Average Pooling（ColSNAP），一次编码生成多级压缩表示，训练时并行优化所有等级，实现在索引阶段按需配置压缩率并保持高检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚交互多模态检索在视觉文档上效果好但每页按patch存储的开销高，且现有压缩方法在索引时固定压缩率，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 在backbone的patch特征上进行空间嵌套平均池化，构建逐级粗化的tier；同时训练所有tier（共享编码一次即可得到所有tier），无需改动模型结构；可在索引时按存储预算选择某一级别编码。

Result: 单模型在较大压缩比下仍能接近全分辨率检索性能；方法可迁移到多种晚交互backbone；大部分收益可通过对预训练检索器的轻量适配实现。

Conclusion: ColSNAP通过在patch网格上构造嵌套的平均池化层，使单个backbone同时产出多级压缩表示，从而在索引时灵活选择存储-准确率折衷，能在大幅压缩下保留近原始分辨率的检索性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal late-interaction retrievers achieve strong retrieval on visually rich documents by representing each page as per patch embeddings and matching at the token level. However, this approach incurs high storage costs. Existing compression methods typically fix a single compression level at indexing time, limiting flexibility. We present ColSNAP (Spatial Nested Average Pooling)1, a training method that generates a nested hierarchy of compression levels directly from a backbone's patch grid. By spatially pooling patch embeddings into pro- gressively coarser tiers and training all tiers simultaneously, a single model learns to support retrieval at multiple compression levels without architectural changes. Crucially, a single encoding pass yields every tier, enabling the accuracy-storage trade-off to be configured at indexing time to match avail- able storage budgets, rather than being fixed during training. We demonstrate that models trained using ColSNAP maintain near full-resolution retrieval performance under substantial compression and that ColSNAP transfers effectively across multiple late-interaction backbones, and achieves most of its improvements via a lightweight adaptation stage applied to a pre-trained retriever.

</details>


### [140] [SearchWiki: Learning to Build and Navigate Knowledge Wikis for Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29953)
*Guransh Singh,Vishwajeet Kumar,Arkadeep Acharya,Adnan Qidwai,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把语料合成为分层 wiki，并用 on-policy RL 训练代理进行多轮导航检索，在多套基准上显著提升检索-生成效果，优于平坦检索方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦检索-增强生成把语料视为块的集合，丢失文档层次与跨文档结构，导致检索失败时难以渐进式细化。提出将语料结构化并训练代理进行多步检索以改善检索质量与生成准确性。

Method: 构建三层 wiki（文档概览、跨文档主题页、页级来源记录），使用工具接口允许代理多轮检索；以 on-policy 强化学习（多成分奖励：答案正确性、检索质量、轨迹效率）优化导航策略；模型为经 RL 调优的 Qwen-9B。

Result: 在 ViDoRe-V3（8 个领域）、FinanceBench 及若干记忆基准（LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-v2）上，WikiResearcher-9B 明显优于同规模无训练基线，并匹配或超越更大外部模型，体现了结构化检索与学习导航的优越性。

Conclusion: SearchWiki 将语料合成为分层、有类型、可导航的 wiki 并训练一个多轮检索代理（WikiResearcher-9B），证明了在结构化语料上学得的导航优于传统的平坦检索增强生成。

Abstract: Flat retrieval-augmented generation treats a corpus as a bag of chunks, discarding document hierarchy and cross document structure. We introduce SearchWiki, a harness framework that synthesizes a corpus into a hierarchical, typed, navigable wiki and trains an agent, WikiResearcher-9B, to retrieve information through multi-turn tool use. The wiki organizes knowledge into three layers - document overviews, cross- document topic pages, and page-level source records; enabling progressive refinement of retrieval when initial lookup misses. We optimize the agent's navigation policy with on-policy reinforcement learning with a multi-component reward function balancing answer correctness, retrieval quality and trajectory efficiency. Evaluation on ViDoRe-V3 (8 domains), FinanceBench, and memory benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, PersonaMem-v2) shows that WikiResearcher- 9B which is our RL-tuned Qwen 9B model, significantly outperforms same-size untrained baselines and exceeds or matches larger external models. SearchWiki paired with WikiResearcher-9B demonstrates that learned navigation over structured corpora is a superior alternative to flat retrieval.

</details>


### [141] [Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29965)
*Nora Girda,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现一种可执行的证据门控机制，将模型生成的数据在未被文档中明确引用与行内一致性证明前标注为可疑并阻止其自动重用，实现在个人健康记录中的可审计边界，示范性评估表明技术上可行但仅验证了完整性路径而非临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纵向健康记录中保存由大语言模型生成的结构化数据会引入完整性风险：似是而非的、但无源证据支持的断言会被后续自动汇总、趋势分析或预防性计算所利用，进而污染病历与决策流程。提出可执行边界以防止模型产出自我授权其后续重用。

Method: 引入“证据门控—信任提升”架构：生成器产生候选结构化数据，但在确认器（deterministic monitor）用文档中唯一支持性引用、同一化验行内字段出现和可追溯性三个硬性条件验证前，数据保持可疑（provisional）；生成器不得自证其词，未通过的候选保留供人工复核。实现于Medical DataCloud并通过自动一致性与变异测试及历史重放评估。

Result: 22项一致性与变异测试全部通过。重放9份历史化验PDF、102行人工标注数据，生成97个数值候选：模式验证通过97/97；早期分包证据检查通过94/97；强化的引用+行级策略放行72/97、将25/97保留为人工审查。研究聚焦系统完整性可行性而非临床正确性或安全性。

Conclusion: 该方法能强制阻断生成器为自身声明的再利用提供授权，从而降低纵向病历的完整性风险；但评估样本和范围有限，仍需处理OCR/格式脆弱性、泛化至更多数据类型与严格的临床验证，以及公开可复现资产以支持外部审计与推广实施。

Abstract: Large language models can convert medical documents into structured data, but plausible output may still be unsupported by the source. Persisting such output in a longitudinal health record, a record that accumulates patient information over time, therefore creates an integrity risk: unverified data may influence later summaries, trends, or preventive-care computations. We introduce an evidence-gated trust-promotion model that keeps generated data provisional until a deterministic monitor verifies it against the source document. The monitor admits a candidate for a specified downstream use only when the source contains a unique supporting quotation, the relevant fields occur within the same laboratory row, and the required provenance is preserved. The generator cannot approve its own output, missing or ambiguous evidence causes refusal, and refused candidates remain available for human review rather than being silently discarded. We implement the model in Medical DataCloud, a personal health-record application, and evaluate it through automated tests and a replay of saved extraction outputs. All 22 conformance and mutation tests pass. The replay covers nine historical laboratory PDF reports containing 102 manually labelled rows. The reports produce 97 numeric candidates: schema validation accepts all 97, an earlier packet-level evidence check accepts 94, and the hardened quotation- and row-level policy admits 72 while retaining 25 for review. The study evaluates system integrity rather than clinical correctness or clinical safety. The results demonstrate the technical feasibility of an enforceable boundary that prevents generated claims from authorizing their own reuse in a longitudinal health record.

</details>


### [142] [EDGE: Engine for Deterministic Graph Evaluation through Conversation Simulation from Graph Structured DSL Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29971)
*Ram Kulathumani,Regunathan Radhakrishnan,Anupam Tripathi,Xiangbo Mao,Roshanak Omrani,Keshav Somani,Shwet Kamal Mishra,Shayna Lurya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于DSL图结构的穷举路径重放评估方法与新确定性/一致性度量，证明显式结构化代理比非结构化代理更具确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理系统演化为复杂的多代理编排工作流，需要系统化框架来衡量代理行为的一致性与确定性，以保证可预测性、可靠性与可调试性。

Method: 基于AgentGraph的DSL，构建可动态调整的有向图表示代理推理；使用图遍历算法穷举会话路径生成完整评估集；重放这些可复现轨迹并比对实际输出与DSL规范；定义并计算新的度量（响应与轨迹确定性、结构遵循性、语义一致性），在精确重放与语言变体下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，使用AgentGraph或LangGraph等显式结构化节点转移的配置，能在确定性度量上显著优于未采用受控转移的代理，验证了所提出评估方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过对AgentGraph定义的有向图进行遍历与重放，可系统性地枚举和评估代理的行为空间，从而量化响应与轨迹确定性、结构遵循性与语义一致性。采用显式结构化节点转移的代理在确定性上优于未受控转移的代理。

Abstract: As agentic systems evolve into complex multi agent orchestration workflows, there is a growing and critical need for systematic frameworks that measures an agent's behavioral consistency and determinism. In this paper, we introduce a formal evaluation methodology that is grounded in AgentGraph, a planner powered by a domain specific language that represents agent reasoning through a dynamically adjustable directed graph. We leverage this structural formalism and utilize graph traversal algorithms that exhaustively enumerate conversational paths, forming a comprehensive evaluation set that captures the agent's complete behavioral space. We then systematically replay these reproducible trajectories to compare observed outputs and state transitions against the intended DSL specification. To quantify reliability, we define novel metrics that measure response and trajectory determinism, structural adherence and semantic consistency across both exact replays and their linguistic variants. Our system's results demonstrate that agents configured using frameworks like AgentGraph and LangGraph with explicitly structured node transitions show superior determinism over agents that are not configured with controlled transitions.

</details>


### [143] [An Open-Source, Event-Driven Pipeline for Cryptocurrency Market Data: Ingestion, Forecasting, and On-Chain Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29973)
*Basil Sajid Shaikh,Melrick Mascarenhas,Nuzhat Faiz Shaikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开放源码、基于Kafka的本地事件驱动管道再现云触发行为，支持高频加密货币数据的存储与分析；进行了比特币价格预测与以太坊欺诈分类的基准实验，明确了比较与评估限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide a reproducible, low-cost alternative to cloud-native, event-driven streaming/warehousing for high-frequency cryptocurrency data and demonstrate end-to-end analytics and modeling over the resulting data marts.

Method: Open-source pipeline on commodity hardware using Apache Kafka and a filesystem-watching poller to emulate cloud event triggers; partition Gemini exchange historical data into hourly/minutely files; asynchronous ingestion via two independent Kafka consumer groups (audit logging and Spark-triggered ETL); cleaned outputs written to PostgreSQL warehouse with historical/aggregated schemas and asset-specific data marts. Modeling: compare seasonal ARIMA vs single-layer LSTM on Bitcoin data mart for forecasting; apply Random Forest and Gradient Boosting with engineered features to the public Ethereum fraud benchmark. Report architecture, methodology, metrics, and limitations.

Result: An end-to-end, fully open-source pipeline that reproduces event-driven behavior on commodity hardware; empirical model comparisons (SARIMA vs LSTM) for price forecasting and tree-based classifiers for fraud detection, with reported metrics and explicit discussion of evaluation limitations.

Conclusion: 实现云式事件驱动管道在常规硬件上是可行的；所做的建模实验有参考价值但受评估设置限制。建议在生产化、评估稳健性（如滚动回测、在线评估）和工程实践（模式注册、容错）上做进一步扩展。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets generate high-frequency, multi-source data that is expensive to work with unless a team already has commercial-grade streaming and warehousing infrastructure in place. This paper describes a fully open-source pipeline that reproduces the behavior of a cloud-native, event-driven system -- file arrival triggering a message, a message triggering compute -- entirely on commodity hardware, using Apache Kafka and a filesystem-watching poller in place of managed cloud triggers. The pipeline partitions historical Gemini exchange data into hourly and minutely files, ingests them asynchronously through two independently grouped Kafka consumers (one for audit logging, one for Spark-triggered ETL), and lands cleaned output in a PostgreSQL warehouse with historical and aggregated schemas plus asset-specific data marts. We use the resulting Bitcoin data mart to compare a seasonal ARIMA model against a single-layer LSTM network for price forecasting, and separately apply Random Forest and Gradient Boosting classifiers, with additional engineered features, to the public Ethereum fraud detection benchmark introduced by Farrugia et al. We report the architecture, the modeling methodology, and the resulting metrics, and we are explicit about the limitations of comparing forecasts issued at different horizons and of evaluating fraud detection on a static, already-labeled dataset.

</details>


### [144] [AutoCRAT: Within-trajectory Joint Control of Stochasticity and Compute for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29988)
*Hanjun Luo,Qiushi Liu,Jingya Zhang,Haihong Pang,Jiaheng Wen,Yifei Ma,Yu Yao,Chengxi Zhang,Hanrong Zhang,Yankai Chen,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在推理过程中联合控制采样随机性和推理预算、仅在语义边界调整离散动作的控制器（AutoCRAT），在减少计算（tokens）同时提升准确率并具备跨模型迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用的静态推理配置无法适应任务和推理阶段的变化，现有自适应方法多只调整探索性或计算量中的一项，缺乏在单条推理轨迹内联合调整二者的机制，因此提出within-trajectory联合控制以提高效率与性能的动态权衡。

Method: 在解码端对冻结的大模型添加控制器；控制器基于解码时可得信号（如置信度、概率分布特征、语句边界检测信号等）在语义边界处选择离散动作，这些动作同时调节采样策略（例如温度、top-k/p）和分配的推理预算（如允许的token数或链式思考步骤）；控制器策略通过离线训练或启发式规则得到，评估在6个基准上与静态和已有自适应方法对比。

Result: AutoCRAT introduces a within-trajectory joint controller for frozen LLM backbones that adjusts sampling stochasticity and reasoning budget at semantic boundaries. It operates over a discrete action space using only decoding-time signals. Empirically, it reduces inference tokens by 13.8–52.7%, improves relative accuracy by 1.5–4.5% over static and adaptive baselines, and transfers across backbones.

Conclusion: AutoCRAT提出的within-trajectory联合控制视角和离散、边界驱动的控制策略是一种有效且实用的方法，能在保持或提升准确率的同时显著节省推理代价，并具有较好迁移性，但需进一步验证稳健性、延迟开销及细节复现性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong reasoning performance, which depends critically on inference-time decisions. Yet these decisions are commonly handled by static, one-size-fits-all policies, limiting adaptation to diverse tasks and reasoning stages. Recent adaptive methods partially address this limitation, but they primarily adapt either decoding stochasticity (how the model explores) or reasoning compute (how long the model reasons) in isolation, leaving their interaction within a single reasoning trajectory unmodeled. To address this challenge, we shift toward a within-trajectory joint control view, and instantiate it in AutoCRAT, a decoder-side controller for frozen backbones. Using only signals available during decoding, AutoCRAT jointly adjusts sampling stochasticity and reasoning budget during generation. AutoCRAT operates over a discrete action space and updates control decisions only at semantic boundaries, improving stability while remaining responsive to the evolving reasoning process. Comprehensive evaluation across 6 benchmarks demonstrates that AutoCRAT (I) uses 13.8-52.7% fewer inference tokens on average than recommended static configurations, (II) surpasses recommended static and adaptive baselines by 1.5-4.5% in relative accuracy, and (III) enjoys strong cross-backbone transferability.

</details>


### [145] [Automatic Conversion of NICE Guidelines to an Executable Computational Model Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30022)
*Ashvin Gupta,Denys Prociuk,Alessandra Russo,Brendan C. Delaney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A stepwise LLM pipeline can convert NICE guideline text into transparent, executable models with good alignment to source guidance and promising performance (F1 82.5%) on limited vignette testing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NICE guidelines are high-quality but unstructured text; existing computable translation methods are disease-specific, labor-intensive, and not scalable. LLMs may automate much of the translation to computable representations.

Method: An end-to-end, stepwise LLM-based transformation pipeline using in-context examples to generate human-inspectable intermediate artifacts and final executable rule models; applied to NICE pancreatic and lung cancer guidelines; evaluated via expert review for rule alignment and tested on 20 pancreatic cancer patient vignettes.

Result: Expert review found strong alignment between source guidelines and generated executable models, with most discrepancies being partial omissions rather than incorrect logic; hallucinated or fundamentally incorrect rules were rare. The executable pancreatic cancer model achieved an F1 score of 82.5% on 20 patient vignettes.

Conclusion: LLMs can transform NICE clinical guidelines from unstructured natural language into interpretable, executable models that preserve guideline structure, enable transparent inspection and modification, and produce patient-specific recommendations; this approach demonstrates feasibility for scalable automated generation of computable clinical guidelines.

Abstract: Introduction: NICE guidelines provide evidence-based recommendations for clinical care but remain largely in unstructured natural language. Existing approaches to converting them into computable representations often focus on individual diseases, require substantial manual encoding, and do not scale. Large language models (LLMs) may enable much of this translation to be automated. Methods: We present an end-to-end approach that converts textual clinical guidelines into executable models capable of generating explainable, patient-specific recommendations. A stepwise LLM-based transformation with in-context examples produces human-inspectable intermediate artifacts. We apply the approach to NICE pancreatic and lung cancer guidelines, use expert review to assess rule alignment, and evaluate the executable pancreatic cancer model on 20 patient vignettes. Results: Expert review showed strong alignment between the source guidelines and generated executable models. Most discrepancies were partial omissions rather than incorrect logic, while hallucinated or fundamentally incorrect rules were rare. On the patient vignettes, the executable model achieved an F1 score of 82.5%. Conclusion: LLMs can transform natural-language NICE guidelines into interpretable, executable models that preserve guideline structure, support transparent inspection and modification, and generate patient-specific recommendations. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of scalable automated generation of computable clinical guidelines.

</details>


### [146] [Interpreting and Steering for Safe and Correct Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30025)
*Hao Yan,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过构建对比数据集并定位与代码安全相关的注意力头与层，本文提出的双向注意力头引导（DuoSteer）在多种漏洞类型上显著降低漏洞率并提升功能正确性，且对不同模型具有可复制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs often produce code with security vulnerabilities; little is known about internal components that drive safe vs vulnerable generations. Understanding these mechanisms could enable targeted, efficient interventions to steer models toward safer code without extensive fine-tuning.

Method: Construct CodeSec-Pairs (9,342 Python safe/vulnerable contrastive pairs) sampled from Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct; perform localization of layers and attention heads related to code safety; design inference-time steering strategies that manipulate attention heads; propose DuoSteer, a double-steering technique that applies both safety and code-correctness steering to attention heads. Validate on five vulnerability types and replicate on Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct using 2,500 pairs.

Result: DuoSteer reduces vulnerability rate by an average of 26.9% and increases functional correctness by 7.5%, outperforming other steering variants, prompting, and supervised fine-tuning baselines. Results also replicate on a second model (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct) with additional contrastive pairs.

Conclusion: Mechanistic interpretation of LLMs coupled with targeted attention-head steering can meaningfully reduce vulnerability in generated code while improving functional correctness; the proposed DuoSteer method is effective and generalizes across models.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate source code containing vulnerabilities, yet little work studies the internal mechanisms that distinguish safe from vulnerable generation in them. In this work, we systematically perform a mechanistic interpretation of LLMs, aiming at both understanding how code safety-vs-vulnerability is represented or driven by components in an LM and turning the insights into actionable steering strategies to encourage safer code generation. To this end, we introduce CodeSec-Pairs, a dataset of 9,342 Python safe-and-vulnerable contrastive code pairs, sampled from Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Utilizing the dataset, we explore approaches to localize layers and attention heads that relate to code safety, and further experiment with different steering strategies for inference-time vulnerability reduction. In particular, we propose DuoSteer, a double-steering approach that simultaneously applies safety and code-correctness steering to attention heads. In experiments over five vulnerability types, DuoSteer leads to an average of -26.9% vulnerability rate reduction and +7.5% functional correctness improvement, which outperforms not only other steering variants but also prompting and supervised fine-tuning baselines. The advantage also replicates on Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct with another 2,500 contrastive pairs sampled from that model.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Uncertainty: Multi-Solver Disagreement Rewards for Self-Evolving Reasoning Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30035)
*Vinoth Selvendran,Zhanming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用一个容量与采样温度各异的模型集成替代单一解算器的不确定性作为Challenger的奖励——用多数答案分布的归一化熵衡量模型间分歧，防止奖励坍缩并发现真正难题，使得下游解算器在多项竞赛数学测试上有稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模型基于采样不确定性的奖励会随着解算器对样本分布变“有信心”而趋于零，无法区分真正容易的问题与仅符合单模型偏好的问题；通过度量模型间分歧，可以直接针对能力边界生成题目，促进解算器学习更稳健的推理策略。

Method: 构建一个在模型容量与采样温度上异质的解算器集成；对每个Challenger生成的问题，对各解算器进行采样得到答案并计算多数答案的归一化Shannon熵，熵值作为Challenger的奖励，鼓励生成导致模型间冲突的问题；替换原有的单模型采样不确定性奖励，保留其余自演化框架不变；在竞赛数学基准上训练并评估。

Result: 提出基于多模型不一致性的奖励函数，通过在异构模型集成上计算每题多数答案的归一化Shannon熵，Challenger能发现使解算器产生冲突的题目，从而生成更具挑战性的课程并提升解算器在竞赛数学基准上的表现（平均+1.34分，使用Qwen3-4B）。

Conclusion: 多模型分歧奖励是一种简单可插拔、无数据外加的信号，可缓解单模型奖励在训练中因答案收敛导致的信号消失，且能推动生成更具广度与鲁棒性的训练题目，从而提升解题模型的泛化能力。不过需进一步验证在不同模型规模、任务域与计算开销下的稳健性与性价比。

Abstract: Self-evolving reasoning frameworks train a Challenger to generate questions exposing a Solver's weaknesses, creating adaptive curricula without human data. However, existing approaches use a single solver's sampling uncertainty as the Challenger's reward. This creates a fundamental bottleneck: as the solver grows confident on the Challenger's question distribution, all sampled answers converge identically, collapsing the reward to zero and starving the Challenger of learning signal. Critically, this single-model reward cannot distinguish genuinely easy questions from those that merely align with one solver's learned biases. We propose a multi-solver disagreement reward using a heterogeneous ensemble varying in model capacity and sampling temperature. A normalized Shannon entropy over the ensemble's per-question plurality answers explicitly rewards questions where solvers produce conflicting solutions---capturing difficulty as inter-model divergence rather than intra-model sampling variance. This richer gradient enables the Challenger to discover questions targeting true capability boundaries, producing a curriculum that forces downstream Solvers to develop robust reasoning strategies generalizing across problem types. Our approach is a drop-in reward function replacement requiring no framework modifications or additional data. Experiments with Qwen3-4B show that Solvers trained on disagreement-Challenger questions achieve +1.34 points average improvement on competition-math benchmarks (MATH-500, AMC, Olympiad), suggesting that multi-solver disagreement provides a complementary and scalable signal for curriculum generation in self-play reasoning systems.

</details>


### [148] [Balance of Benchmarks: Semantic Density Reweighting for Benchmark Multiplicity and Task-Conditioned Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30044)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoB 用语义逆密度加权+残差场实现对基准列表的重复鲁棒性与任务条件化排名，比均等加权更能发现任务特定强项并抵抗基准复用偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前常用的均等加权会把发表密集的基准区域反复计入整体能力评估，从而造成隐含偏向。作者希望通过语义逆密度加权把这种偶发的基准分布效应显式化并校正，同时提供任务条件化的模型排名工具。

Method: （1）对基准描述进行语义嵌入，计算每个基准在嵌入空间的局部密度；（2）对每个基准施加逆密度权重，权重平衡由披露的密度尺度控制；（3）把异构性能分数等价映射到共同潜在量表；（4）构建残差场：在相同几何结构下，基于任务查询对模型的性能偏差进行条件化调整；（5）在586模型、14基准上进行评估，使用 profile correlation 与 Kendall tau 测量任务预测能力与对重复基准的鲁棒性。

Result: 提出了 Balance of Benchmarks (BoB) 方法，通过将基准任务描述嵌入语义空间并对基准按语义密度取逆权重，来抵消多次重复或高密度覆盖区域对聚合评分的不当影响。BoB 将异构分数映射到共同的潜在量表，再使用同一几何结构的残差场（residual field）在给定任务查询时对模型排名进行调节。实证上，在586个模型与14个基准数据集的快照上，BoB 在预测模型在留出任务上表现的异常强项时，profile 相关为0.462（均等加权为0.049）；在对基准重复抗性测试中（每个基准复制4次），BoB 排名与原始排名的 Kendall tau 为0.995（均等加权为0.936），表明对重复基准更稳健，同时残差场可用于任务条件化预测。

Conclusion: BoB 将基准列表的组成从评价套件的偶发属性转变为可控的测量设计要素，提供了一种既能抑制密集重复基准影响又能基于任务查询调整模型排序的原则性方法。

Abstract: Language models are commonly compared by averaging scores across a benchmark list with equal weight. Such lists grow through publication outside an explicit measurement design, so equal weighting turns the density of published benchmarks into an implicit capability weight: densely benchmarked regions count repeatedly. We introduce Balance of Benchmarks (BoB), which embeds benchmark descriptions and assigns each benchmark an inverse-density semantic weight. Nearby entries share aggregate influence at a disclosed density scale. After equating heterogeneous scores onto a common latent scale, a residual field uses the same geometry to condition model rankings on a task query. The two components serve distinct empirical roles. On a snapshot of 586 models and 14 benchmarks, BoB predicts which models are unusually strong on a held-out task beyond their general ability, reaching a profile correlation of 0.462 compared with 0.049 under equal weighting. It also limits the influence of densely repeated benchmarks on the aggregate. After adding four copies of each benchmark in turn, the resulting rankings retain a Kendall tau of 0.995, compared with 0.936 under equal weighting. The residual field therefore provides task-conditioned prediction, and inverse-density weighting provides robustness to benchmark multiplicity. Together, they turn benchmark-list composition from an incidental property of evaluation suites into an explicit, controllable part of measurement design, providing a principled foundation for task-aware and multiplicity-robust model evaluation.

</details>


### [149] [Can LLM Agents Discover? Evaluating Creativity on ML Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30047)
*Shitanshu Bhushan,Yunxiang Zhang,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出三维创造力评价并构建LLM评判流水线，发现当前LM代理能产生相对历史新颖的方案但难以将新颖性转化为更高任务性能，且单次运行中新颖性随时间下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宣称能实现“自主科学发现”的大模型研究代理是否真正具备创造力——即在新颖性（相对自身和相对人类历史）与有用性两方面产生兼顾的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于三维（P-Creativity、H-Creativity、Usefulness）的评估框架；以10个来自MLE-Bench的Kaggle风格ML工程任务为测试床，对AIDE和AIRA-Dojo两种代理进行多轮轨迹采样；构建“LLM作为评判者”的自动化评价流水线并与人工判断进行相关性验证，用以在规模上评估P-Creativity。

Result: LLM评判器与人工评分高度相关，可用于规模化P-Creativity评估；所有代理在从探索向利用过渡时P-Creativity下降；代理在H-Creativity上超越获奖人类解法但在任务性能上落后；总体表明代理擅长发现新颖解但难以将其转化为更好结果。

Conclusion: 当前多轮LLM研究代理能够探索出与人类历史解法不同的解空间（高H-Creativity），但在把这些新颖解法转化为更高任务性能方面能力不足；在单次运行内（P-Creativity）代理会从探索阶段向利用阶段转变时新颖性下降。

Abstract: Recent AI systems promise autonomous scientific discovery, claiming to discover algorithms and produce research papers, yet understanding whether they exhibit creativity, the capacity to produce solutions that are both novel and useful, remains an open question. We present a framework for evaluating multi-turn LLM research agents' creativity using ML engineering tasks as a testbed, through three dimensions: P-Creativity (psychological novelty: novel relative to the agent's own prior solutions within a run), H-Creativity (historical novelty: novel relative to the corpus of human solutions), and Usefulness (task performance). Evaluating two agent frameworks, AIDE and AIRA-Dojo, on 10 Kaggle-style machine learning tasks from MLE-Bench, we develop an LLM-as-a-Judge pipeline and verify its strong correlation with human creativity judgments, providing a reliable automated metric for P-Creativity evaluation at scale. Applying this pipeline to agent trajectories, we find: (1) all agents exhibit declining P-Creativity as they transition from exploration to exploitation; (2) LLMs exhibit greater H-Creativity than medal-winning humans, yet achieve lower performance. Our findings reveal that current agents can explore novel regions of the solution space but lack the capacity to convert this novelty into improved task performance.

</details>


### [150] [Spec2Twin-Chain: Orchestrating Bi-Level Optimization with LLMs for Blockchain Digital Twin Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30050)
*Haoting Zhang,Haoxian Chen,Jiayuan Sheng,Donglin Zhan,Zeyu Zheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An LLM-guided, bi-level automated pipeline (Spec2Twin-Chain) constructs and calibrates blockchain digital twins via iterative architecture proposals and simulation-based parameter optimization, retaining evaluated designs to improve proposals and supporting downstream policies.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reduce manual, non-reusable effort in constructing blockchain digital twins by automating translation from specifications and behavioral evidence into simulator architectures and calibrated parameters.

Method: Upper level: LLM generates and revises structurally admissible architectures from system specs, evidence, and feedback. Lower level: simulator-based optimizer tunes parameters under explicit objectives and constraints. Iterative interaction, archive of evaluated candidates guides proposals; validated via controlled experiments (calibration, recovery, stress tests, policy optimization/updating).

Result: Spec2Twin-Chain: a bi-level optimization framework where an LLM proposes/revises admissible simulator architectures (upper level) and a simulation-based optimizer calibrates architecture-conditioned parameters (lower level); iterative loop with a global archive of evaluated candidates; experiments show behaviourally accurate twins, iterative improvement via feedback, and reuse for downstream decision making.

Conclusion: Framework is promising: it automates twin construction, improves designs through iterative feedback, and enables reuse for policy decisions; but it needs more analysis on robustness, scalability, hallucination control, and reproducibility details.

Abstract: Building a blockchain digital twin largely requires translating domain knowledge and specific system descriptions into a simulator architecture, calibrating its parameters against behavioral evidence, and validating the constructed twin. These steps are commonly performed through application-specific modeling efforts that can be difficult to reuse across systems and downstream decision problems. We consider automating this process through Spec2Twin-Chain, a framework that formulates blockchain digital-twin construction as a bi-level optimization problem. At the upper level, a large language model proposes and revises structurally admissible architectures using system specifications, behavioral evidence, and feedback from evaluated designs. At the lower level, a simulation-based optimizer calibrates the architecture-conditioned parameters under explicit objectives and guardrail constraints. The two levels iterate. The evaluated candidates at lower levels are retained in a global archive and used to guide subsequent proposals at upper levels. We conduct controlled experiments involving twin calibration, feedback-driven recovery, stress analysis, downstream policy optimization, and policy updating. The results demonstrate that the framework can construct behaviorally accurate twins, improve initial designs through iterative feedback, and reuse calibrated twins to support downstream decisions.

</details>


### [151] [Mitigating Over-Optimization in PRM-Guided Search in Mathematical Reasoning by Optimizing the Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30051)
*Taejong Joo,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 PRM 导致的极值噪声问题，作者提出了一个基于 maximin 鲁棒优化的训练免费搜索策略，有理论支撑并在大量实验中显著提升了 PRM 引导搜索的稳健性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近来的工作表明，PRM 引导的基于搜索的推理会因过程奖励不完美而过度优化，导致可行轨迹被剪枝、虚假轨迹被扩展；作者旨在从理论上刻画这种失效并提出一个鲁棒的搜索策略以减轻该问题。

Method: 理论分析了直接使用 PRM 得分受验证器噪声影响的极值效应，基于此把搜索过程表述为在一组可行奖励扰动上的 maximin 鲁棒优化；实现为一种训练免费（no fine-tuning）的过程监督方法，用以在评分噪声下保留替代解并降低过拟合高分异常路径的影响。

Result: 在多种设置下（16 个实验配置），maximin 搜索在不做微调或在线调整的条件下，平均提升 17–35% ，并在 14/16 个情形中优于基于结果或逐步得分的基线方法；同时降低对 PRM 异常高分的敏感性。

Conclusion: 提出的 maximin PRM-guided search 通过将 PRM 引导的搜索视为对可行奖励扰动的鲁棒优化（maximin）问题，能在无需微调或在线适应的前提下缓解验证器噪声导致的极值效应，从而保留有前景的候选前缀并减少对被过度优化的 PRM 异常值的敏感性。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) provide dense step-level guidance for search-based reasoning, enabling inference-time compute to be allocated toward promising partial solutions. However, recent evidence suggests that PRM-guided search can over-optimize imperfect process rewards, pruning viable trajectories while expanding spurious ones. In this work, we theoretically show that directly leveraging PRM score is vulnerable to verifier noise through an extreme-value effect: non-viable prefixes become more likely to receive spuriously high scores as reasoning depth increase. Therefore, we formulate the PRM-guided search as a robust optimization problem over plausible reward perturbations, termed maximin PRM-guided search, leading to a training-free robust process supervision method that preserves promising alternatives when step-level scores are noisy. Maximin PRM-guided search mitigates this failure mode by reducing sensitivity to over-optimized PRM outliers. Without fine-tuning or online adaptation, maximin search consistently improves the PRM-guided search by 17-35\% on average, outperforming outcome- and step-level baselines in 14 out of 16 settings. Our source code is available at https://github.com/tjoo512/maximin-search.

</details>


### [152] [Game-Agnostic Value Functions through Automatic JSON Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30056)
*Dien Nguyen,Diego Perez-Liebana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于 JSON 描述的通用轨迹表示（JSON-Bag）并用于训练游戏无关的价值函数；通过随机森林与阶段选择优化特征，实验证明特征选择是性能关键，所提出方法在多款桌游上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种通用、可扩展的状态表示与价值估计方式，使得基于 JSON 的游戏描述可被自动化地转换为可学习的特征，并降低为每款游戏手工设计表示的成本。

Method: 将游戏状态/轨迹的 JSON 表示分解为标记（tokens）并构造原型（prototypes）；使用随机森林进行特征重要性评估并做全局与阶段特异性特征选择；将筛选后的特征用于训练价值函数，并在 One-step-look-ahead 策略下对六款桌游进行对比实验与统计分析。

Result: 提出了 JSON-Bag VF：一种基于 JSON-Bag 原型的通用、与游戏无关的价值函数训练方法；结合随机森林特征选择与关卡/阶段特异性特征选择；在六个桌游上以 One-step-look-ahead (OSLA) 策略评估，多个原型-标记化与特征选择组合中，JSON-Bag OSLA 在多数游戏上优于基础 OSLA；分析表明特征选择对性能提升最为关键，超过原型标记化的影响。

Conclusion: JSON-Bag VF 是一种有效的游戏无关价值估计方法，且随机森林驱动的特征选择（含阶段特征）显著提高性能；总体上，特征选择比原型化/tokenization 对性能影响更大。

Abstract: JSON Bag-of-Tokens (JSON-Bag) is a recently proposed method to generically represent game trajectories by tokenizing their JSON descriptions. We introduce JSON-Bag VF, a game-agnostic approach to training value functions for game-playing agents using JSON-Bag prototypes. We show that this approach can be enhanced with Random Forest-based feature selection and a method to select game-stage-specific features. We evaluate JSON-Bag VF with One-step-look-ahead (JSON-Bag OSLA) on six tabletop games over different combinations of prototype-tokenization and feature selections. JSON-Bag OSLA outperforms baseline OSLA agents in most games. Our analysis also shows that feature selection significantly improves JSON-Bag VF and that feature selection is the most important factor in JSON-Bag VF performance, over prototype-tokenization.

</details>


### [153] [VERA: Authority-Preserving Edge Revocation for Federated AI-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30091)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了可由验证者检查的撤销合同VERA，形式化了在有“或”授权（disjunctive authority）下的精确撤销目标，给出实现并在多套代理框架数据上做了可移植性与完整性实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多代理系统中委托以有向图方式组合，传统的token或子树无效化方式不足以精确反映撤销影响，导致误撤或漏撤；因此需要一个可验证、可由运行时检查的精确撤销合同来维护授权一致性。

Method: 形式化定义撤销目标T_intent(e,G)=reach(G)\reach(G\{e})，在代理运行时通过adapter签发可验证的撤销合同与API。实现上在LangGraph等框架中生成编译后追踪（traces）与签名的委托决定，并对目标证明与拒绝未授权签名/省略攻击进行验证。

Result: 该文通过提出VERA（一种可验证的边撤销合同与API）来解决代理运行时中的授权一致性问题，关键定义了目标集合T_intent(e,G)=reach(G)\reach(G\{e})，能精确表示在删边时应撤销的代理集合。作者实现并在多框架和可复现追踪上验证了方案，声称能避免树级级联误撤、支持跨部署场景并对签名与省略攻击给出防护。

Conclusion: VERA在理论上通过目标集合T_intent精确刻画了边撤销影响，实验证据（若按作者陈述）显示对树级撤销的过度和不足两类问题都有缓解，并能跨若干现有多代理框架迁移；但论文摘要含有大量表述与数据格式错误，需澄清模型、实验细节与安全假设以增强可信度。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks compose planners, tool agents, remote services, and shared specialists into runtime delegation graphs, but their revocation APIs still resemble token or subtree invalidation. When one delegation is withdrawn, the runtime must know which agents lose authority while independently authorized agents keep working. We study this authority consistency problem and introduce VERA (Verifiable Edge Revocation for Agents), a verifier-checkable revocation contract and API emitted by agent-runtime adapters as signed evidence. Under disjunctive authority, revoking edge e invalidates exactly T_intent(e,G) = reach(G) \ reach(G \ {e}), the agents whose every authorizing root path used e. Used as a contract, this target exposes two runtime failures: tree cascades over-revoke shared agents, while deployer-scoped cascades under-revoke cross-domain descendants. In a LangGraph framework-replt cells repeated 20 times yield 500compiled-framework traces and 2,000 valid signed delegation decisions; 13/25 cells contain runtime multi-parsharing and 8/25 contain cross-deployer shies 500/500 target proofs, preserves all320 alternate-parent shared-agent cases that tree cascade revokes, and rejects unauthorized signers and omission attacks. Baseline replay over 1,9that holder/node and tree-style targetscannot express this behavior. We further validate schema portability on A2A, AutoGen, and CrewAI artifacts: nine traces, including five executable Cregned delegation events that pass schema and signature checks.

</details>


### [154] [A.X K2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30181)
*Cheolseung Baek,Dhammiko Arya,Eunki Kim,Gun Song,Gyoungeun Han,Hyunho Yang,Hyunjun Eun,Jin Kim,Junyoung Park,Juyun Wee,Minki Hong,Minkyung Park,Minsang Kim,Minsoo Kang,SaeRom Kim,Sangjin Kim,Sangyeol Lee,Seojin Lee,Seokhwan Jo,Seokyoung Hong,Seongho Choi,Seonghye Cho,Seongmin Ok,Sereimony Sek,Seungmo Cho,Seungsik Kim,Singon Kim,Sohee Park,Sooyeon Park,Subin Yi,Sungbin Yoon,Sungeun Lee,Sung Jun Cheon,Sungwan Kim,Sunwoo Lee,Tae Yoon Kim,Wonbeom Jang,Yohan Ra,Yong-jin Han,Youngjin Kim,Youngrang Kim,Yujin Kang,Yujin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A.X K2 是为 agentic 场景优化的 688B MoE 模型，借助稀疏门控注意力与门控归一化在更短训练 tokens 下显著提升长上下文能力、量化友好性与下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为需要长上下文、代理行为和程序化交互的应用提供一个高效、可部署的基础模型；提升 token 效率、长上下文适应成本、低位量化可用性和多模态/多语言竞争力。

Method: 从零训练 688B 参数 MoE，约 8.5T tokens，使用更高质量且扩展的 agentic/软件工程数据；引入 Sparse Gated Attention（SGA，稀疏 + gated 注意力）与 Gated Norm（GN）用于训练稳定性；采用稀疏 indexer warmup 以原生支持 128K 上下文（每次查询仅读取 2,048 个位置）；实现 4-bit NVFP4 量化并保持接近 FP8 的精度；提出 Think-Fusion 切换“思考/非思考”模式。

Result: 相比 A.X K1 在多个 benchmark 上有系统性提升（某些项超 30 个百分点）；在 256K 上下文 RULER 得分 94.6；稀疏 indexer 每查询仅读 2,048 个位置但长上下文质量不降；GN 令 4-bit NVFP4 在服务端表现接近 FP8（差异约 1 分）；在数学与韩语基准与强开源权重基线相当或优于之。

Conclusion: 提出了 A.X K2——一个 688B 参数的 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）语言模型，作为面向 agentic 应用的高性能基础模型。通过更优的数据混合和架构改进，在训练 tokens 更少的情况下显著超越前代 A.X K1，并在长上下文、量化和多任务性能上取得实用性增强。

Abstract: We introduce A.X K2, a 688B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model trained from scratch as a high-performance foundation for \emph{agentic} applications. Trained on approximately 8.5T tokens---fewer than its predecessor, A.X K1---on a smaller but higher-quality mixture with substantially expanded agentic and software-engineering data, it nonetheless improves over A.X K1 across the board, by over 30 percentage points on some benchmarks, reflecting large gains in token efficiency. To support long contexts efficiently, we introduce Sparse Gated Attention (SGA), which combines sparse attention with gated attention, and adopt Gated Norm (GN) to stabilize large-scale training. SGA is trained natively at 128K through a \emph{sparse} indexer warmup that optimizes the indexer against its own sparse top-$k$ selection rather than the dense attention distribution, making adaptation markedly cheaper: each query reads only 2,048 positions, yet long-context quality is unchanged and A.X K2 scores 94.6 on RULER out to 256K. The outlier suppression of GN in turn keeps 4-bit NVFP4 serving within one point of FP8 accuracy. A simple yet effective Think-Fusion recipe further lets users switch between thinking and non-thinking modes within a single unified model. Extensive evaluations show that A.X K2 performs competitively against strong open-weight baselines, matching or exceeding them on math and Korean-language benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [FaVOR: LLM-Based Agentic Framework for Factor Mining via Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30192)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Minseok Kim,Seunghyeon Jung,Sujin Pyo,Huisu Jang,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FaVOR把因子发现从单纯追求收益转向基于可观测证据验证经济假说，通过分解—验证—整合的agentic流程，提高因子可解释性与跨周期鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化因子挖掘直接以收益为优化目标，导致生成的因子在数学形式上与原始经济假说脱节，产生伪相关并在 regime 转换时失效。

Method: 提出一种三阶段的一致性循环：分解（将经济假说拆成可观测条件）、验证（检查每个因子是否反映相应条件）、整合（合并为结构可解释的复合因子）；整体以agentic框架执行，使发现过程以假说证据为目标而非直接优化收益。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500上的实证中，FaVOR优于现有基线方法，并在不同市场环境下保持有效性；代码已开源。

Conclusion: FaVOR通过在因子挖掘流程中持续约束数学形式与经济假说的一致性，能够生成可解释性、抗周期性且经济含义明确的因子。

Abstract: Traditional finance relies on experts to hand-craft factors through a principled process grounded in economic rationale. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have automated this process, scaling factor mining far beyond manual effort. However, these automated approaches optimize directly for returns and rarely check whether a generated factor still expresses the economic hypothesis that motivated it. We identify this inconsistency between mathematical form and economic meaning as a structural failure mode of return-oriented automation. The resulting factors blur the line between real signals and spurious correlations and break down across regime shifts. We propose FaVOR (Factor Validation through Observable Reasoning), an agentic framework that restructures factor mining around hypothesis-level evidence rather than return outcomes. In place of the standard hypothesis-to-formula leap, FaVOR enforces a three-stage consistency loop tying mathematical form to economic rationale throughout. (1) Decomposition splits a broad economic hypothesis into independent observable conditions. (2) Validation checks whether each factor reflects its intended condition. (3) Integration merges them into a composite whose structure remains interpretable. On the CSI 500 and S&P 500 in 2025, FaVOR outperforms existing baselines while remaining effective across regimes. FaVOR shows that hypothesis-grounded factor discovery produces signals that are interpretable by construction, regime-robust, and economically faithful. The code is available at https://github.com/damilab/FaVOR.

</details>


### [156] [SPARK: Skeleton-Guided Reasoning Synthesis from Large-Scale Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30214)
*Yu Li,Wei Li,Xin Gao,Mengyuan Sun,Xiaoyang Wang,Qizhi Pei,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Scientific reasoning remains challenging for open-source models, largely due to the lack of high-quality scientific reasoning data. Existing datasets are often dominated by factual recall or formulaic problem solving, with limited emphasis on mechanism understanding, evidence-grounded reasoning, and hypothesis evaluation. To address this, we introduce SPARK (Scientific Paper Abstracted Reasoning sKeleton), a paper-oriented synthesis framework built on Sci-Base, a large-scale corpus of research papers spanning 10 scientific disciplines. Instead of directly converting papers into question-answer pairs, SPARK treats the claim-evidence-derivation structure of a paper as the fundamental unit of reasoning synthesis. Specifically, SPARK (1) distills each paper into a compact reasoning skeleton capturing its central claims and supporting evidence, enabling self-contained question generation, and (2) synthesizes reasoning tasks from four scientific perspectives: mechanistic reasoning, hypothesis falsification, quantitative derivation, and boundary calibration. A final consistency verification stage further removes unsupported or contradictory outputs. Using this framework, we construct Spark-234K, a scientific reasoning dataset with substantially higher difficulty and diversity than existing resources. Experiments show that Spark-234K consistently outperforms existing scientific reasoning datasets while achieving stronger performance with significantly fewer training samples.

</details>


### [157] [LaMoC: Loss-Aware Modular Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30226)
*Mohanad Odema,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LaMoC：通过将激活统计与经验费舍尔（Empirical Fisher）按梯度-误差一致性进行混合，实现基于损失感知的模块级压缩统计选择，从而改进联合模块化压缩并带来小幅但稳健的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合模块化压缩主要依赖激活统计，忽视了与下游任务损失相关的敏感性信息。目标是引入模块级的损失感知信息（通过经验费舍尔）并与激活信息融合，使压缩决策更能反映对下游损失的影响。

Method: 对每个模块同时计算激活统计与经验费舍尔（通过在数据上计算梯度的二阶近似信息），定义梯度-误差对齐量度并作为压缩统计的选择依据。将问题表述为两层优化：内层最小化模块重构误差，外层搜索/调整激活与梯度信息的混合率。用经验驱动的流程加上统计验证来选择最终压缩方案并报告性能提升。

Result: LaMoC blends activation statistics and Empirical Fisher via gradient-error alignment to select compression statistics per module, formulated as a two-tiered optimization and solved with empirical/statistical validation. Empirically it yields an average 2.5% perplexity reduction and 1% relative task accuracy improvement on 4–8B models across four model families (eight models) versus state-of-the-art modular compression.

Conclusion: 经验费舍尔可作为模块级损失感知代理，与激活统计混合并通过两层优化调节混合率，能够使局部重构误差更好地对齐下游损失，带来困惑度和任务精度的可度量改进；但需进一步评估计算开销、超参敏感性及对更大模型和更多任务的泛化性。

Abstract: Modular compression has enabled considerable parameter reduction in LLMs while preserving strong language understanding and downstream task accuracy. However, existing joint modular compression methods primarily rely on activation statistics, leaving loss-sensitivity information and its module-level characterization underexplored. We investigate addressing this gap with LaMoC, a loss-aware modular compression methodology that blends activation and Empirical Fisher statistics through gradient-error alignment. LaMoC improves joint compression by selecting compression statistics that better align local module reconstruction error with the downstream loss. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) We characterize the Empirical Fisher as a module-level loss-aware proxy that can be blended with the activation statistics required for compression. (2) We reformulate joint modular compression as a two-tiered optimization problem that minimizes module reconstruction error while tuning the activation and gradient information blending rate. (3) We implement an empirically driven methodology with statistical validation to solve the resulting compression problem. We evaluate LaMoC across four model families spanning eight models. On the 4-8B models, LaMoC achieves an average 2.5% reduction in perplexity and a 1% relative improvement in task accuracy over state-of-the-art modular compression methods.

</details>


### [158] [Rethinking the Test-Time Prompt Tuning Objective from the Perspective of Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30230)
*Jungwon Choi,Hyeonseo Jang,Kibok Lee,Eunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用基于增强视图构造的、经过置信度感知温度缩放的目标分布替代传统的熵最小化；对原始视图预测施加与该目标分布的交叉熵损失，同时对目标分布的熵进行对抗性处理，以在有可靠证据时提高置信度、在不确定或冲突时保留不确定性，从而提升精度与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时提示调优(Test-time prompt tuning, TPT)通常通过对多视图增强结果进行熵最小化(EM)来微调提示，但该做法会使模型偏向过度自信的预测，忽视样本特有的不确定性，从而显著恶化模型的校准性能。需要一种能在提高准确率的同时保留或恢复样本级不确定性的目标函数。

Method: 对每个测试样本生成多种增强视图并得到各自预测；根据每个增强视图的置信度（例如最大概率或熵）对其预测施加置信度感知的温度缩放：置信度高的视图使用较低温度（使分布更尖锐），置信度低的视图使用较高温度（使分布更平滑）。将这些经温度缩放的视图预测聚合为一个目标分布（例如加权平均）。用该目标分布与原始视图预测计算交叉熵作为主要适配损失，同时对目标分布的熵做对抗性引入以保留样本特有的不确定性（防止适配过程导致不合理的置信度膨胀）。通过在测试时仅微调提示参数来最小化该复合目标，从而改进准确率与校准。

Result: 提出的目标通过用来自增强视图的目标分布（经置信度感知的温度缩放）与原始视图预测做交叉熵配对，并对目标分布的熵进行对抗性引入，从而在多项基准上同时实现了最先进的准确率和明显更好的校准性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了EM带来的过度自信问题，使模型仅在目标分布可靠时才提高置信度、在目标不可靠或矛盾时保留不确定性，最终在准确率与置信度校准之间实现更好的折衷。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) has emerged as a powerful paradigm, refining prompts for each test sample via entropy minimization (EM) over multiple augmented views. However, we identify a limitation in the standard EM-based adaptation: it inherently drives the model toward overconfident predictions disregarding sample-specific uncertainty, leading to significant calibration degradation. To address these limitations, we propose a new objective that replaces the conventional EM loss by aligning the original-view prediction with a target distribution derived from augmented views via cross-entropy, while adversarially incorporating the entropy of the target distribution to capture sample-specific uncertainty. Furthermore, to better construct this target distribution, we apply confidence-aware temperature scaling to each augmented-view prediction according to its confidence, sharpening confident predictions while softening uncertain ones. This formulation allows the model to increase confidence only when the target distribution is reliable, while preserving uncertainty when it reflects ambiguous or conflicting augmented-view predictions. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also significantly improves model calibration.

</details>


### [159] [CoLa-ICD: A Knowledge-Enhanced Framework for Long-Tail Automated Medical Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30234)
*Yihang Cheng,Veronica Liesaputra,Andrew Trotman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 知识增强的标签扩展+代码依赖建模+标签-证据对齐，有效提升长尾ICD自动编码性能并达成SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动医疗编码面临文档过长、标签分布极不平衡以及术语多样性问题；尤其是少数样本（长尾）代码易被语义相近标签混淆，亟需利用外部知识与依赖关系增强标签表达并改进对齐以提升长尾预测。

Method: 提出一个知识增强框架：1) 用外部术语（ontology/词表）扩展每个ICD标签的语义表示；2) 建模相关代码之间的依赖（例如通过图/注意力机制）；3) 强化标签语义与临床文本证据的对齐（例如对比或跨模态匹配），并在长文档和不均衡标签设置下训练。

Result: 在若干基准上（标签规模增大且稀疏时）CoLa-ICD明显改善了长尾代码的预测性能，整体在AUC、F1与P@k上取得SOTA成绩；作者并开源了代码。

Conclusion: CoLa-ICD通过引入外部术语扩展标签语义、建立代码间依赖建模并强化标签与临床证据之间的对齐，有效缓解长尾ICD预测问题，在更大更稀疏的标签空间中提升效果并达到AUC、F1、P@k的最新水平。

Abstract: Automatic medical coding assigns ICD codes to clinical notes, but it remains challenging due to long documents, imbalanced label distributions, and diverse terms. These challenges are especially severe for rare codes, which have limited training instances and are easily confused with semantically similar labels. We introduce CoLa-ICD, a knowledge-enhanced framework for long-tail prediction. CoLa-ICD enriches ICD labels with external terms, models dependencies among related codes, and learns stronger alignment between label semantics and clinical evidence for long-tail prediction. Experiments show that CoLa-ICD improves long-tail prediction with larger gains in larger and sparser label spaces and achieves state-of-the-art performance in AUC, F1, and P@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/youwillbethebest/Cola-ICD.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Completion Combining Discrete Structural Coding with Similar Entity Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30235)
*Jiaqi Wang,Dongying Lin,Yang Yang,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoSC 通过“结构编码+相似实体细化”的两阶段流程，利用 LLM 生成并借助结构相似实体重排候选，从而在 FB15k-237 上提升了 KGC 的排名指标（尤其是 MRR 与 Hits@10）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的 KGC 方法两条主线：把图结构编码为离散 token 或只在受限候选集上做精修，且二者多独立研究。作者认为将两者结合可兼得 LLM 的生成能力与结构相似实体带来的局部判别信息，从而改善预测质量。

Method: 方法包括两步：1）离散结构编码：将 KG 的结构信息转换成离散 token 序列供 LLM 输入，LLM 输出初始候选实体排序；2）基于相似结构实体的细化：检索与查询实体结构相似的实体，利用这些实体的信息（如其结构特征或实体表征）对初始排序进行重评分或重排序，得到最终候选列表。

Result: 在 FB15k-237 数据集上，CoSC 在 MRR 和 Hits@10 上优于现有基线方法，在 Hits@1 上保持竞争力，表明结合策略能提升整体排序质量且对前排精确率影响有限。

Conclusion: CoSC 将离散结构编码与相似实体信息结合，先由 LLM 基于结构编码生成候选实体排序，再用结构相似实体信息对排序进行细化，从而提高知识图谱完备任务的效果。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion requires models to use both textual descriptions and relational structure. Existing LLM-based methods either encode KG structure as discrete tokens or refine a restricted set of candidate entities, and these two directions have largely been studied separately. We propose CoSC for LLM-based KGC, which combines discrete structural coding with similar entity information. Specifically, an LLM generates an initial candidate entity ranking from discrete structural codes, after which information from entities with structures similar to that of the query entity refines the ranking. Experiments on FB15k-237 show that CoSC outperforms existing baselines on MRR and Hits@10 while remaining competitive on Hits@1.

</details>
