### [161] [Generating Workflow DAGs from Natural Language with Non-Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30250)
*Anand Iyer,Bhanu Khetharpal,Srinivas Upadhya,Ramkumar Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把组合性强的图装配交给确定性编译器、用学习型注册表缩小生成范围，低成本非推理LLM也能以生产就绪质量生成复杂路由DAG，实测在合成基准上显著提升有效性并降本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用单体大提示让LLM一次性输出完整JSON DAG会遇到“发射密度瓶颈”：模型能选对节点但难以正确配置大量相互依赖的属性与布尔组合。通过把组合性转移到编译器并约束生成词汇，可以缓解这一瓶颈，降低对昂贵推理模型的依赖。

Method: 将自然语言到图的映射分为两部分：1) 学习型前端负责选择注册表项并生成简洁中间表示（IR），限定输出词汇与局部结构；2) 一个确定性编译器把IR组装为目标平台的JSON DAG，处理并行分支、先中断回退链以及分支上的布尔谓词。评估在一个635条规则的合成基准上，比较四个模型与一个推理模型的表现，并用LLM判定器与精确匹配指标衡量质量与合法性。

Result: 作者提出一种神经符号分解方法，将组合图构建从生成模型转移到确定性编译器，并用学习驱动的注册表选择前端约束生成词汇，从而高效地将自然语言路由规则转为可执行DAG。该方法在合成635条规则基准上，达到约89% LLM评价有效性、约90%精确匹配条件准确率、99–100% JSON合法率，并在提示令牌数上节省约一半。

Conclusion: 神经—符号分解在结构化生成场景中能显著降低模型的推理负担、提高正确性与效率；但结果依赖合成数据、注册表质量与编译器设计，尚需在真实业务规则、可扩展性和错误分析上进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of translating natural-language routing rules written by business administrators into executable workflow graphs for enterprise contact centers. Each target is a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of conditional actions with parallel branches, hit-first fallback chains, and per-branch Boolean predicates, encoded in the JSON dialect of a commercial routing platform. We show that neuro-symbolic decomposition enables lower-cost, non-reasoning large language models to generate complex workflow DAGs at production-relevant quality without expensive extended-reasoning models. Our central diagnostic is an emission-density bottleneck: on a 635-rule benchmark of manufactured synthetic data, models select the correct graph nodes with high accuracy but increasingly misconfigure attributes and Boolean grouping as the number of interdependent nodes emitted in one pass grows. We therefore move combinatorial graph construction from the model into a deterministic compiler driven by a compact intermediate representation, with a learned registry-selection front end that focuses generation on relevant vocabulary. Across four models, the full system reaches approximately 89% LLM-judge validity, approximately 90% exact-match condition accuracy, and 99-100% valid JSON while using roughly half the per-rule prompt tokens of a monolithic prompt. On GPT-5.3-chat, the method improves judge validity by 24 percentage points and achieves statistical equivalence to a reasoning model's out-of-the-box quality, although an approximately 8-point frontier gap remains. We also present a deployment path and transferable lessons for structured-generation applications.

</details>


### [162] [SimCRAFT: Distilling Remote Sensing Agents via Synthetic Trajectories and Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30277)
*Haoran Wang,Jing Yao,Xu Yang,Zeqing Wang,Yang Zhang,Pedram Ghamisi,Zhengchao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过合成约束数据与检索增强微调，把遥感工作流编排能力压缩到 7B 模型，得到接近闭源性能的开源轻量级代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥感代理依赖大规模通用 LLM，缺乏深度领域知识且部署/推理成本高；需要轻量级、具专业能力且易部署的开源替代方案。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 用多智能体合成引擎结合 Mock Execution Engine 自动生成、约束验证的大规模工作流语料 SimRS-14k；2) 提出 Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning（CRAFT），将检索到的标准操作流程（SOPs）在噪声鲁棒目标下用于类比推理式微调，从而避免机械复制并扩展到多步遥感工作流规划。模型无关，可在三种 7B 骨干上重现。

Result: SimCRAFT-7B 在多项实验中显著优于现有开源大模型，并逼近闭源强模型与专用遥感代理的性能，展示了在资源受限条件下的高效自主部署可行性。

Conclusion: SimCRAFT 成功将遥感工作流编排能力蒸馏到 7B 级别的小型模型上，通过 SimRS-14k 数据集和 CRAFT 微调策略，在资源受限环境下提供可竞争的开源遥感智能基线。

Abstract: The unprecedented surge in Earth observation data volume and diversity has exposed a critical bottleneck for traditional manual workflows, catalyzing the emergence of Remote Sensing (RS) Agents. However, the practical deployment of these advanced agents is severely hindered by their heavy reliance on large-scale general-purpose LLMs, which lack deep domain expertise and impose prohibitive infrastructure demands. To resolve this, we propose SimCRAFT, a model-agnostic framework that distills sophisticated RS orchestration capabilities into a compact 7B-scale model. Addressing data scarcity, we first pair a multiagent synthesis engine with a Mock Execution Engine that checks schema correctness, inter-tool dependencies, and sensor/tool compatibility, producing SimRS-14k, a large-scale, constraint-validated workflow planning corpus. Second, we propose Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (CRAFT) that finetunes the model to reason analogically by adapting retrieved Standard Operating Procedures to novel queries under a noise-robust objective, generalizing RAFT to multi-step RS workflow planning without mechanical copying. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SimCRAFT-7B significantly outperforms openweights LLMs and rivals advanced closedsource models and specialized RS agents, while reproducing across three 7B backbones. This work contributes a competitive open-weights baseline for lightweight RS intelligence, enabling efficient autonomous deployment under resource-constrained or resource-conserving conditions.

</details>


### [163] [Ignorance or Incompetence? Constructing Knowledge-Gated, Verifiable Tasks for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30322)
*Hanlin Tian,Minhao Li,Yu Mi,Sihan Zhu,Zhao Yang,Yuxiang Wang,Hongquan Zhu,Qiufei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将任务说明与私有工件分离的知识门控构造协议，通过溯源、泄露审计与可执行见证使对私有约定的依赖可检验；实验证明代理在有工件时性能提高且在无工件时可能完全失败，公开了部分资源，但未断言这些任务能提升训练后能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实专业代理任务常依赖公共语料库中缺失的私有约定，而现有基准很少控制代理能否接触这些约定。需要一种可使对私有知识依赖显式化并可测试的构造与评估方法，以提高基准和系统评估的有效性与透明度。

Method: 设计并实现知识门控任务构造协议：
- 将任务指令与紧凑工件分离；工件携带私有约定、参考表和效用操作符；
- 在任务构造时记录溯源，保证在提供/撤去工件条件下任务指令字节完全一致；
- 通过泄露审计、可执行见证和五次试验的经验门控筛选来验证对工件的依赖；
- 使用确定性求解器和规则语料库为结构化任务提供精确真值；对无法由单一可执行判定器检查的输出，使用命名的准则级评分细则；
- 在15个校准任务上评估若干代理配置，实施配置相对的校准筛选，保留满足五次试验门控的任务。

Result: 在15个校准任务中：一个前沿代理配置在提供工件条件下通过率为68.0%，在撤去工件条件下为0%；某一任务在提供一个看似合理但错误的工件时五次试验均为0%；最终经配置相对校准筛选后保留了7个满足五次试验门控的任务。确定性求解器与规则库为结构化任务提供了精确真值，论文同时公开了部分任务与工具。作者强调这些实验验证了构造协议的行为，而非证明保留任务能改善训练后性能。

Conclusion: 该论文提出并验证了一种“知识门控”的任务构造协议，使任务说明与包含私有约定、参考表和操作符的紧凑工件分离，从而把对私有约定的依赖变为显式且可检验的属性。实验表明若提供工件，代理表现显著提升；若撤去，表现可降为零。该协议通过构造时间溯源、字节一致的说明、多重泄露审计和可执行见证增强可验证性，但作者明确不声称保留的任务能提高模型的训练后性能。部分任务及工具已公开。

Abstract: Professional agent tasks often depend on conventions that are absent from public corpora, yet benchmarks rarely control whether an agent has access to those conventions. We introduce a knowledge-gated task-construction protocol that separates a task instruction from a compact artefact containing private conventions, reference tables, and utility operators. Construction-time provenance, byte-identical task instructions across the provided- and withheld-artefact conditions, leak audits, and executable witnesses make dependence on the artefact explicit and testable. Across fifteen calibration tasks, one frontier agent configuration achieves a 68.0% pass rate with the artefact and 0% without it; on one task, a plausible but incorrect artefact also yields 0% across five trials. Deterministic solvers and rule corpora provide exact ground truth for structured tasks, while named criterion-level rubrics support outputs that cannot be checked by a single executable oracle. A configuration-relative calibration screen retains seven tasks satisfying our five-trial empirical knowledge-gating screen. These experiments validate the behavior of the construction protocol; they do not establish that the retained tasks improve post-training. We publicly release part of the task suite and supporting tooling at https://github.com/DatagridsAI/Knowledge-Gated-Task-Construction.

</details>


### [164] [Answer Probing-Guided Search for Diverse Solution Exploration of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30345)
*Yi Fang,Que Shen,Chengpeng Li,Boyi Deng,Wei Shi,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Fengli Xu,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在中间推理层“探测”潜在答案并利用其隐藏态与困惑度，APTS能更准确地区分和筛选不同解法路径，从而在树搜索中生成更多多样且高质量的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理时容易聚焦于单一高置信解，限制了对其他有效解法路径的探索；现有用响应级语义嵌入去去重的方法易被语言表述/风格相似性混淆，无法可靠区分真正不同的解法路径。

Method: 提出Answer Probing：在树搜索的中间节点上让LLM生成（或“探测”）该路径最终可能得到的答案，抽取探测答案的隐藏态表示以衡量路径间的语义差异，并使用探测答案的困惑度作为推理正确性的代理指标。基于此设计APTS算法，用隐藏态相似性去裁剪或扩展分支，并以困惑度优先级调整搜索策略。

Result: 在三个推理任务上、两种LLM的实验证明：1) 用探测答案的隐藏态比直接的语义嵌入更能区分不同解法路径；2) 探测答案的困惑度与路径正确性相关，可作为实用的排序/筛选信号；3) APTS在多样性指标上较基线方法有一致提升，表明方法有效且鲁棒。

Conclusion: APTS（Answer Probing-Guided Tree Search）通过对中间推理路径“探测”潜在答案，并利用探测答案的隐藏状态相似度与困惑度（perplexity）来引导树搜索，从而在多任务与多模型设置下稳定提高生成解的多样性与质量。

Abstract: Generating multiple diverse and high-quality solutions is valuable for many applications, such as code-test generation and drug discovery. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to converge on a single high-confidence solution during inference, limiting exploration of alternative valid solution paths. Existing test-time methods promote diversity through tree-like search and prune semantically similar branches using response-level semantic embeddings. However, we find that such embeddings are easily confounded by linguistic and stylistic similarities, making it difficult to distinguish genuinely distinct solution paths. To address this, we introduce Answer Probing, which probes the potential answer an LLM would reach from an intermediate reasoning path. We demonstrate that the hidden states of probed answers more effectively differentiate distinct solution paths than semantic embeddings, and the perplexity of probed answers serves as a practical proxy for reasoning correctness. Based on these findings, we propose Answer Probing-Guided Tree Search (APTS), which guides the tree search by the probed answers' hidden state similarity and perplexity. Experiments on three reasoning tasks across two LLMs show that APTS consistently enhances solution diversity, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [165] [Co-Annotator: Expert-Distilled ViT and VLM for Visual and Documentation Guidance in Age-Related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30352)
*Ziheng "Leo" Li,Benjamin Freeman,Akshay Raman,Kavin Aravindhan Rajkumar,Xinxin Fang,Rishabh Srivastava,Steven Feiner,Kaveri A. Thakoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distilling expert gaze and dictation into complementary visual and language guidance yields substantial, non-additive efficiency gains in retinal OCT interpretation, addressing both search and documentation bottlenecks while preserving accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clinical AI often optimizes predictive metrics but ignores how clinicians allocate visual attention and compose documentation; guiding visual search and automating structured documentation could address two workflow bottlenecks.

Method: Collect expert gaze and dictations to train (1) a gaze-aligned Vision Transformer that predicts fixation-aligned AOIs, and (2) an ontology-bounded vision-language model that pre-fills editable biomarker summaries. Evaluate with three user studies: US1 training data collection and model training, US2 controlled resident study testing each modality separately, and US3 combined deployment across two institutions testing both modalities together.

Result: US1: models trained on expert data improved diagnostic accuracy and biomarker generation. US2: AOI guidance produced lasting perceptual-efficiency carryover; VLM guidance doubled biomarker documentation breadth; each modality safe and independently beneficial but did not improve in-guidance efficiency. US3: combined guidance increased correct diagnoses per minute by 40% and reduced comment editing time by 67% without compromising diagnostic accuracy; combined in-guidance efficiency gains notable.

Conclusion: Expert-distilled multimodal guidance (gaze-aligned AOIs + ontology-bounded VLM prefilled biomarker summaries) can simultaneously reduce visual search overhead and documentation burden in retinal OCT interpretation without degrading diagnostic accuracy.

Abstract: Clinical AI often optimizes predictive performance without engaging how clinicians decide where to look and what to write. We present Co-Annotator, which distills expert gaze and dictation into two guidance components: a gaze-aligned Vision Transformer producing fixation-aligned areas of interest (AOIs), and an ontology-bounded vision-language model (VLM) that pre-fills editable biomarker summaries for retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT). We first collect expert gaze and dictations (US1) to train the models, significantly improving diagnostic accuracy and biomarker generation. We then deploy the system with ophthalmology residents: a controlled resident study (US2) confirmed each modality is safe and independently beneficial, with AOI guidance producing lasting perceptual efficiency gains through post-guidance carryover and VLM guidance more than doubling biomarker documentation breadth. In a combined deployment across two academic institutions (US3), providing both modalities simultaneously produced efficiency gains that substantially exceeded either modality alone: correct diagnoses per minute increased by 40% and comment editing time fell by 67%, without compromising diagnostic accuracy. Notably, neither modality improved efficiency during guidance in US2, which makes the in-guidance efficiency gain under combined guidance in US3 the more striking result. Expert-distilled multimodal guidance can remove two distinct clinical workflow bottlenecks at once (visual search overhead and documentation burden) without compromising the diagnostic accuracy clinicians already achieve.

</details>


### [166] [Will the User Ever Know? Covert Indirect Prompt Injection on Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30362)
*Yunseok Lee,Yunji Kim,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Decompose ASR into detectable (OSR) and undetectable (CSR) successes; find ReAct format causes the split; propose ICoA to steer agents back and increase covert successes; show CSR gains on AgentDojo.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard ASR metric ignores whether a user can notice a successful IPI in the agent's final response. The authors aim to measure and increase truly stealthy (covert) attacks that leave no trace for the user, and to understand structural causes of covert vs. overt outcomes.

Method: Trajectory analysis of successful IPI traces to categorize covert vs. overt outcomes; formal decomposition of ASR into CSR and OSR; design of ICoA attack that executes injection then steers agent back to the user task; empirical evaluation on four target models in AgentDojo comparing CSR/OSR against baselines.

Result: Empirically, ICoA attains the highest CSR on AgentDojo across four models, improving CSR by 3.79–12.01 percentage points relative to the strongest baseline. Analysis shows covert traces hand control back to the user task, while overt traces end at the attack, linked to the ReAct final-summary behavior.

Conclusion: The paper identifies that Attack Success Rate (ASR) hides whether users can detect indirect prompt injection (IPI) and proposes decomposing ASR into Covert Success Rate (CSR) and Overt Success Rate (OSR). It shows that the ReAct response format causes covert vs. overt outcomes and introduces ICoA, an attack that purposefully induces covert outcomes by steering the agent back to the user task after executing the injection, achieving higher CSR across models.

Abstract: As LLM agents take real-world actions through tools, indirect prompt injection (IPI) has emerged as a serious threat. The standard metric, Attack Success Rate (ASR), counts whether an injection succeeds but ignores what the user notices in the agent's final response. Looking at successful injection traces, we find two distinct outcomes: the agent executes the injection while returning an otherwise normal response, or reports the injected action in its final response, giving the user a chance to notice. We call these covert and overt successes. From the user's perspective, we decompose ASR into the Covert Success Rate (CSR), counting successes leaving no trace in the final response, and the Overt Success Rate (OSR), counting successes the user can detect. To understand what drives the gap, we analyze successful trajectories and find that the agent's behavior after the injection separates covert from overt: covert traces hand control back to the user task before ending, while overt traces end at the attack itself. This split follows from the ReAct format, where the final response summarizes the most recent action. Building on this observation, we propose ICoA (Induced Covert Attack), an IPI attack designed to induce covert outcomes by steering the agent back to the user task after executing the injection. Across four target models on AgentDojo, ICoA achieves the highest CSR, with gains of 3.79-12.01 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [167] [Augmenting Human Performance with an XR Agent Learning from Online Behavior and BCI Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30369)
*Ziheng Li,Xichen He,Haoyan Chen,Charlie Zou,Sheng Bai,Benjamin Yang,Mengyuan Wu,Jake Ledner,Yi-Jie Cheng,Akito Yamauchi,Dishita G Turakhia,Steven Feiner,Paul Sajda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OLIVE 在线融合被动 EEG 与行为信号，无需离线标签地自适应冻结 VLM，在 XR 实验中显著提升实时目标识别与交互的速度与可靠性，且对任务突变的恢复更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类感知与动作带宽成为瓶颈的时序性与高风险场景中，单靠显式行为可能无法及时识别并响应环境变化，研究如何利用隐式生理信息扩展用户的检测与交互能力，从而在关键时刻提供可信的实时辅助。

Method: 在 XR 第一人称射击任务中，冻结视觉-语言模型（VLM）推理，将用户的显式行为信号（被击落的目标）与隐式生理信号（基于注视锁定的 EEG）进行在线融合；通过联合估计各信号源的可靠性（无需手动标签或离线训练）来修正模型关于任务相关性物体的推断，实现测试时适配与连续自适应。

Result: 通过三项用户研究（含两次实地 XR 部署），OLIVE 在与既有测试时适配框架的比较中实现帕累托优势：以可比的收敛速度达到最高收敛率；结合行为与生理信号时，在会话内对用户检测与击中目标的提升最大且更稳定；当目标静默切换时，含生理信号的代理较仅行为信号代理收敛更快（平均 1.27 倍，p = .008）。

Conclusion: OLIVE 能在无离线标签或训练的前提下，通过在线融合被动 EEG 与行为信号来实时调整冻结的视觉-语言基础模型，从而在时序紧迫、高风险和动态任务中提供更可靠和更快收敛的辅助。

Abstract: We present OLIVE, a framework for adapting a foundation model to provide real-time assistance in temporally demanding, high-stakes, and dynamic tasks. We show that passive EEG, fused online with behavioral evidence, can meaningfully extend the number of targets users detect and engage beyond their unaided action bandwidth. OLIVE learns from both explicit behavioral signals (the targets the user shoots down in an XR first-person shooter game) and implicit physiological signals (fixation-locked EEG) to provide timely guidance, continuously adapting a frozen vision-language model's inference on which items are task-relevant by jointly estimating per-source reliability without manual labels or offline training. Through three user studies, including two live deployments of an assistive agent driven by OLIVE in XR, we show that OLIVE Pareto-dominates prior test-time adaptation frameworks, achieving the highest convergence rate at comparable convergence speed. Combining implicit physiological and explicit behavioral signals, the OLIVE agent produces the largest and most reliable within-session improvement to a user's ability to detect and engage targets, largely independent of the individual's skill. When the target switches silently, the agent that uses both behavioral and physiological signals reconverges significantly faster than the behavior-only agent (1.27 times faster on average, p = .008), restoring trustworthy guidance at the moment the task changes, precisely when reliable assistance matters most.

</details>


### [168] [Scaffolding Foundation Models into Physical-World Agents Pushes the Frontier of Long-Horizon Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30396)
*Zixing Lei,Gengze Zhou,Xiong-Hui Chen,Jiazhao Zhang,Yiyang Huang,Hang Yin,Haoqi Yuan,Qi Wu,Weixin Li,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NavMCP用三通道把VLM决策者与NFM执行器耦合，形成无需重训练的持久长航程代理，显著提升模拟与真实机器人上的探索与问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型在长时任务上能力分裂：VLM擅长高层推理但在反复导航落地时脆弱且低效；NFM在语义目标的闭环执行上稳健但作为有界回合缺乏跨回合、任务级的持续推理。需要一种方法把两类模型的互补能力耦合以支持长航程实体代理。

Method: 提出一种agentic scaffolding框架：VLM作为高层决策者，决定要寻求的证据、搜索位置和停止时机；NFM作为闭环导航执行器，把VLM下发的语义子目标转为安全鲁棒的导航轨迹。三条协作通道——intent（把证据需求翻译为导航调用）、observation（将导航回放转为带来源的轨迹证据）、memory（跨回合积累发现、负证据和未决目标）—实现持续性交互。该方案不需要对任一基础模型做额外训练。

Result: 在模拟任务（HM-EQA、MT-HM3D、EXPRESS-Bench）上实现SOTA，且在与相同骨干的基线对比中，在HM-EQA上超出片段化接口14.9个百分点；在真实平台Unitree Go2上达到78.3%成功率，且随任务时域增长，优于最强基线的优势从10点扩大到45点。

Conclusion: NavMCP通过把推理型视觉-语言模型（VLM）与执行型导航基础模型（NFM）用三条通道（意图、观测、记忆）耦合，能够在不重训练任一模型的前提下把孤立的导航回合转化为持久的具身长时任务交互，从而在模拟和真实机器人上都显著提升长航程探索与问答任务的表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon physical-world agents must reason over distant goals while grounding decisions in reliable closed-loop behavior. Today's foundation models split these capabilities: vision-language models (VLMs) infer missing information and adapt high-level plans but remain brittle and inefficient at repeated navigation grounding, while navigation foundation models (NFMs) robustly execute semantic goals but operate as bounded episodes without persistent task-level reasoning. We introduce NavMCP, an agentic scaffolding framework that couples a VLM reasoning agent with an NFM executor for long-horizon exploration. The VLM decides what evidence to seek, where to search, and when to stop, while the NFM grounds each semantic sub-goal into closed-loop navigation. Three channels structure their collaboration: intent translates evidence needs into navigation calls, observation converts rollouts into source-grounded trajectory evidence, and memory accumulates findings, negative evidence, and unresolved goals across calls. This design turns isolated navigation rollouts into persistent embodied interaction without retraining either model. On Embodied Question Answering, NavMCP achieves state-of-the-art results on HM-EQA, MT-HM3D, and EXPRESS-Bench. Under matched agent and executor backbones, it outperforms an episodic interface by 14.9 percentage points on HM-EQA. On a Unitree Go2, NavMCP reaches 78.3% success, with its margin over the strongest baseline growing from 10 to 45 points as the task horizon increases. These results demonstrate the potential of scaffolding complementary foundation models into long-horizon physical-world agents.

</details>


### [169] [Dense Clinical Contrasts Enhance Medical Knowledge Updating in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30405)
*Yangmin Huang,Shu Quan,He Geng,Xin Ye,Qianyun Du,Zhiyang He,Jiaxue Hu,Xiaodong Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Supervision format matters: EMQ (elimination-style multiple-choice with distractors) best supports temporally-anchored medical knowledge updates under equal training budget, improving retention and transfer compared to MSQ, FITB, SAQ.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether the format of supervision affects the effectiveness of medical knowledge updates for LLMs when budget is controlled, addressing risks of models relying on outdated but plausible clinical knowledge.

Method: Matched-budget supervised fine-tuning (SFT) comparison across four supervision formats (EMQ, MSQ, FITB, SAQ) on temporally-anchored oncology staging updates; introduced SEER-Bench benchmark and evaluated on HealthBench Professional; diagnostic analyses of representation change and signal density.

Result: EMQ supervision yields the most stable external transfer and retention among same-budget SFT variants; updated 4B model with EMQ reached 64.8% answer accuracy and 59.6% rationale accuracy on SEER-Bench; EMQ preserves discriminative representations with smaller parameter/representation movement and offers denser clinical contrast signals.

Conclusion: Medical knowledge updating depends not only on update algorithms but importantly on how knowledge is structured as supervision; choosing dense-contrast formats like EMQ can improve update efficacy and retain base-model behaviors with less representational drift.

Abstract: Medical knowledge changes continually, making large language models vulnerable to relying on outdated yet clinically plausible information. We study whether the format of supervision affects medical knowledge updating under a matched training-budget setting. We introduce SEER-Bench, a temporally anchored oncology-staging benchmark curated from the latest versioned SEER Research Data release, and render identical medical update events from NCCN oncology guidelines into four supervision formats: EMQ, MSQ, FITB, and SAQ. Across SEER-Bench and HealthBench Professional, EMQ gives the most stable external transfer and retention among same-budget SFT variants. With EMQ supervision, the updated 4B model produces competitive results on temporally anchored oncology staging, reaching 64.8% answer accuracy and 59.6% rationale accuracy on SEER-Bench. Diagnostic analyses suggest that EMQ exposes denser clinical contrast signals while preserving discriminative representations with smaller movement from the base model. These results show that medical knowledge updating depends not only on the update algorithm, but also on how knowledge is structured as supervision.

</details>


### [170] [DERELAB: Probing Defeasible Reasoning and Confirmation Bias in LLMs with a Generative Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30413)
*Jayanta Sadhu,Sayem Shahad,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeReLab提供了可验证的多轮可撤销推理测试；评估显示大模型普遍存在确认偏差且不善于基于反证信息调整信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于可撤销（非单调）推理的数据集静态且覆盖面不足，难以控制地考察模型在接收新增证据时的信念更新行为。需要一个可控、可验证的测试床来系统研究模型如何处理确认与反证证据。

Method: 基于参数化图结构生成包含默认推理和继承推理的多轮对话。每一轮都有经形式验证的标准答案，以便在受控设置下测量模型对确认/反驳证据的响应。将该生成器用于设计隔离特定推理需求的实验，并在九个大模型上评估确认偏差表现。

Result: 在九个公开和闭源的大模型上评测，几乎所有模型表现出系统性偏向：接受一致证据、抗拒不一致证据；若证据只是削弱原结论，若干模型能识别但仍不修改结论。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了DeReLab生成框架，能够产生具备形式化逐轮真实答案的多轮信念更新对话，用以评估可撤销推理。实验显示多数大语言模型存在确认偏差：更容易接受与当前结论一致的证据，而抗拒不一致的更新；部分模型能识别“削弱”信息但未相应修正结论。

Abstract: Defeasible reasoning is a type of reasoning where inferences are drawn from plausible current evidence, but can be retracted upon the introduction of newer evidence. Although recent studies have examined language-model behaviors in defeasible reasoning, the datasets have been static and lack wide coverage of non-monotonic reasoning categories. We introduce DeReLab, a generative framework that produces multi-turn belief-updating conversations from parameterized graph structures across default and inheritance reasoning, with formally verified ground truth at every turn, enabling controlled measurement of how models respond to confirming and disconfirming evidence. This controlled generation process creates a testbed for experimental designs that isolate specific reasoning demands. Applying this capability to the study of confirmation bias, we evaluate nine open and proprietary large language models and find that nearly all exhibit a systematic tendency to accept congruent evidence while resisting incongruent updates, with several models correctly identifying a weakening update yet failing to revise their conclusion. We believe our work and findings will facilitate future research on evaluating language models in defeasible reasoning.

</details>


### [171] [From Metaheuristics to Exact Methods: A CP-SAT Approach for Multi-Objective Healthcare Workforce Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30419)
*Vipul Patel,Anirudh Deodhar,Dagnachew Birru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CP-SAT 是一套面向多角色多技能护理排班的可证合规的 CP 公式化，支持细粒度和多种实际需求，且在多种实例上表现出良好可扩展性与显著服务质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗传/整数/约束方法在班次粒度仅建模6–12项约束，无法保证法规合规，且缺乏跨技能/跨角色、强制休息中点控制、按病情加权公平、次班次粒度和跨零点班次等实用功能。

Method: 基于约束规划（CP-SAT）构建模型：引入移位窗口（shift-window）分解以实现带中心性控制的强制休息排班，采用网格偏移预处理（grid-offset）将跨零点班次映射到单一排班日；以 14 项硬约束和 15 项软目标构成统一加权惩罚函数，支持15分钟到1天的多粒度分辨率和重心/中点控制、按病情权重的工作量均衡及周间稳定性。

Result: 在18个实例（合成病区、INRC-II 基准、NRP-23 兼容实例）上构造性实现零硬约束违规；在 INRC-II n005w4 上证明最优（目标118，Gap 0%，104s）；能生成规模达179,800变量/351,425约束（80名护士）的可行解；与 MOGA 比较服务质量提升50–67%；模型规模按人线性增长约为≈4,400变量/员工；模型总约束29项（14硬、15软）。

Conclusion: CP-SAT 提供了一个保证零硬约束违规、支持多角色多技能和多细粒度的护理人力排班约束规划模型，并在多组基准上展示了可扩展性和较高服务质量提升。

Abstract: Healthcare workforce scheduling is an NP-hard optimization problem requiring simultaneous satisfaction of labor regulations, coverage requirements, employee preferences and cost objectives. Existing approaches (genetic algorithms, integer programming, constraint programming) model 6-12 constraints at shift-level granularity and cannot guarantee regulatory compliance. They also lack support for multi-role, multi-skill heterogeneity, mandatory break scheduling with midpoint control, acuity-weighted workload equity, sub-shift granularity, inter-week stability, and cross-midnight shifts.
  This paper presents CP-SAT: a Constraint Programming formulation for multi-role, multi-skill healthcare scheduling. CP-SAT enforces 14 hard constraints guaranteeing zero regulatory violations, while optimizing 15 soft objectives via a unified weighted penalty function. Contributions include a shift-window decomposition enabling break scheduling with centrality control, acuity-weighted workload equity, multi-granularity resolution from 15 minutes to 1 day, inter-week stability, and grid-offset preprocessing mapping cross-midnight shifts into a single scheduling day without solver changes.
  CP-SAT is evaluated on 18 instances: five synthetic hospital units (10-33 nurses), 10 INRC-II benchmarks (5-80 nurses, up to 8-week horizons) and 3 NRP-23 compatible instances (10-25 nurses) with cross-midnight Night shifts. Results: zero hard-constraint violations across all 18 instances by construction; proven optimality on INRC-II n005w4 (objective 118, gap 0.0%, 104s); feasible schedules scaling to 179,800 variables and 351,425 constraints (80 nurses); service quality improved 50-67% over MOGA; and model size scaling near-linearly at approximately 4,400 variables per employee. The formulation enforces 29 total constraints (14 hard, 15 soft), nearly three times the industry average.

</details>


### [172] [EvoSkill Injection: Red-Teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30429)
*Doyun Kim,Chanwoo Kim,Sugyeong Eo,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种针对自演化代理技能管线的红队方法SARGE，构造了两个基准并展示了恶意技能注入及其持久激活的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化代理通过自动生成、精炼和重用技能实现持续能力演化，但这一自动化流程带来了新攻击面：恶意技能可能被自动生成、储存并反复复用，因此需要专门的评估框架和基准来揭示与衡量此类风险。

Method: 本文定义了EvoSkill Injection威胁模型，并提出SARGE红队框架：通过迭代的生成—升级—强化交互流程诱导自演化代理形成恶意技能；构建EvoSkillBench（恶意交互轨迹基准）用于训练/诱导攻击；设计EvoSkillSafetyBench用于评估注入后技能的检索与激活情况。

Result: 实验表明SARGE可以成功诱导恶意技能形成；这些被注入的技能在技能库中持久存在并在后续任务中多次被激活，证明了能力腐化的可行性和持久性。

Conclusion: SARGE能够诱发自演化代理生成并长期保存恶意技能，注入的技能会在后续任务中被检索并触发，表明自演化技能管线存在显著的持久性能力破坏风险。

Abstract: LLM-based agent systems increasingly adopt skill-based architectures to reduce repetitive reasoning costs and improve stable, efficient task execution. Recent studies propose self-evolving agents that autonomously generate, refine, and reuse skills from past experiences to enable continuous capability evolution. However, autonomous skill evolution introduces a new attack surface in which malicious capabilities are generated, stored, and reused as legitimate skills. In this paper, we define EvoSkill Injection as a threat model targeting the autonomous skill generation and evolution pipeline of self-evolving agents. We further propose SARGE (Red-teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in self-evolving agents), a red-teaming framework for evaluating this threat model through iterative generation, escalation, and reinforcement interactions. To support our framework, we construct EvoSkillBench, a benchmark dataset of malicious interaction trajectories for inducing malicious skill formation in self-evolving agents, and introduce EvoSkillSafetyBench, a post-attack benchmark for evaluating whether injected malicious skills are subsequently retrieved and activated as harmful behaviors. Our evaluation shows that SARGE induces malicious skill formation and that injected skills are persistently stored and repeatedly activated, highlighting the risk of persistent capability corruption.

</details>


### [173] [CHASE: How Content Ecosystems Are Reshaped When Ranking Is the Only Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30466)
*Qianwen Gao,Zichang Su,Yiwen Hou,Arlen Kumar,Leanid Palkhouski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHASE模拟显示：固定LLM排序信号下的重复优化会导致内容朝排名特征集中，降低与独立质量评判的一致性并产生领域相关的同质化与激励扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在LLM驱动的检索系统中，创作者群体持续优化内容以迎合生成模型排名信号时，整个内容池的长期群体效应（例如同质化、质量与可见性对齐破坏）如何演化，目前缺乏系统的实证或模拟分析。

Method: 提出CHASE（一种受控模拟框架），用排序作为可见性代理，先验证排序与在生成式回答中被引用之间的相关性，然后在六个领域进行迭代实验：在每轮对文档进行特征判别、重写并按固定LLM排序器重新评估，重复20轮，并加入随机目标作为对照。

Result: 在六个领域中，质量-排名一致性均下降（Spearman rho从R0到R20的变化范围为-0.107到-0.018，均值-0.068）。排序与被引用的关联在六个领域的rank-citation AUC为0.853±0.093。对照实验表明变化是由面向排名的适应而非单纯迭代重写导致，且动态表现高度依赖领域。

Conclusion: 反复针对固定LLM排序信号进行优化，会改变内容生态，使得创作者越来越偏向排名特征而非独立质量评判，从而降低质量-排名的一致性并引发领域相关的同质化和激励扭曲。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is increasingly used to improve content visibility in LLM-based retrieval systems, yet its population-level effects under repeated optimization remain poorly understood. We introduce Content Homogenization under rAnking Signal Exploitation (CHASE), a controlled simulation framework for studying how content ecosystems are reshaped when creators repeatedly adapt documents to an LLM ranking signal. We use ranking as a proxy for source visibility and validate this abstraction against citations in grounded generated responses, obtaining a rank-citation AUC of 0.853 $\pm$ 0.093 across six domains. CHASE then iterates ranking, feature discrimination, rewriting, and evaluation over 20 rounds across different domains. Quality-ranking alignment decreases in all six domains: from R0 to R20, the change in Spearman's rho ranges from -0.107 to -0.018, with a mean change of -0.068, which means documents closer to the ranking feature profile become less aligned with independently judged document quality over the simulation horizon. A random-target control has shown that it is associated with adaptation toward ranking-derived incentives rather than iterative rewriting alone. The resulting ecosystem dynamics are strongly domain-dependent. Together, these findings show how repeated optimization against a fixed LLM ranking signal can reshape both content populations and the incentives faced by content creators.

</details>


### [174] [CM2: Multimodal Cultural Reasoning via an Integrated Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30498)
*Qi Li,Zhaojie Kang,Yingjie He,Zheng Lin,Hao Zhang,Guangxin Wu,Yan Gong,Rong Fu,Jianyuan Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于人类文化解读路径的多智能体多模态框架CM2，结合RAG、网络化推理与奖励反馈，实验证明在跨模态文化推理上优于CoT与常规模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在纵向的符号化、步骤化STEM推理上表现突出，但在横向的、跨学科的文化理解与解释（需要融合模态信息与背景知识并进行仲裁）方面欠缺专门机制，因而提出以人类文化解读认知路径为蓝本的系统化方案以填补该空白。

Method: CM2由若干模块组成：多模态感知模块提取视觉/文本特征；检索增强生成（RAG）用于引入外部文化背景知识；网络化多智能体推理实现横向文化论证和仲裁；门控融合器整合多源证据；基于任务反馈的奖励机制优化决策。整个框架在多个MLLM骨干模型上进行端到端或模块化集成。

Result: 在CM2D数据集与若干文化推理任务上，CM2相较于链式思维(CoT)和常规模式化推理展现稳定增益；消融实验显示各模块均对绩效有贡献；冲突分析表明系统能进行真实的跨模态仲裁决策而非仅靠单一模态主导。

Conclusion: CM2提出了一种以人类文化解读路径为基础的多智能体多模态框架，通过感知-检索增强生成-网络化推理-门控融合-奖励反馈的流水线，实现了在跨模态、跨学科文化推理任务上的性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown remarkable success in STEM domains, where progress is often driven by vertical, step-by-step deduction under relatively stable symbol systems. Their horizontal, interdisciplinary cultural reasoning, however, remains underexplored.We propose CM2, a multi-agent framework grounded in the cognitive pathway of human cultural interpretation. CM2 integrates multimodal perception, retrieval-augmented generation, networked reasoning, gated fusion, and reward-driven feedback.Experiments on CM2D across multiple MLLM backbones show consistent gains over CoT and typical reasoning paradigms; ablations validate each module's contribution, and conflict analyses confirm genuine cross-modal arbitration.

</details>


### [175] [ScienceArena: Benchmarking LLMs on Latest Scientific Olympiad Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30517)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Wenpu Liu,Yaoming Li,Linfeng Hao,Shuyang Hou,Zijian Guo,Xinrui Zhang,Yuntian Zhao,Zhengyang Wang,Wenrui Liu,Yuhan Wu,Tong Yang,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceArena是面向奥赛类开放题的专家审计基准，使用奖牌得主验证与LLM评判校准来扩展可评估性；结果表明LLM可在若干考试上获得奖牌级别分数，但视觉与长程一致性问题限制了更广泛的可靠推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准受数据污染与饱和影响，难以反映最前沿LLM在真实科学推理场景下的能力；奥赛题提供开放式、多步、以过程记分为主的挑战，更能揭示模型在科学问题解决中的薄弱环节。

Method: 从十三个公开的科学竞赛（含IPhO、IChO、IBO、USAPhO、USNCO等）收集题目、图表、官方解答与评分细则，通过专家审计的数字化流水线将这些材料结构化，随后由奥赛奖牌得主验证。为扩展标注规模，对LLM担任评分者进行了基于历史模型答案与奖牌得主真值的校准，并在此基础上评估十四个近代大模型。

Result: 两个经校准的LLM评分器在总分上与专家保持一分内的差距；评估显示若干顶尖模型能在部分公开国际试题上达到奖牌等效的记分成绩，但化学题与长时程一致性仍为显著瓶颈。专家批注指出失败多源于视觉理解、题目结构复现与整体解题流程控制，而非专业术语匮乏。

Conclusion: ScienceArena构建了一个针对物理、化学、生物三科奥赛级别开放性、多步推理题的基准，能更真实地评估模型的科学推理能力，并在避免数据污染方面具有优势。LLM-as-judge在经校准后可在总分上接近人类奖牌得主，但视觉、结构一致性和全局问题控制仍然是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Benchmark saturation and data contamination increasingly obscure genuine scientific reasoning in frontier LLMs. We introduce \textsc{ScienceArena}, an olympiad-style benchmark from thirteen public science competitions in physics, chemistry, and biology, including IPhO and IChO 2025--2026, IBO 2023, USAPhO 2026, and USNCO 2025. Its open-ended, multi-step problems use process-credit rubrics, making faithful scoring difficult. We build ScienceArena through an expert-audited digitization pipeline that converts official exams, figures, solutions, and rubrics into structured items verified by olympiad medalists. To scale evaluation beyond costly human grading, we calibrate LLM-as-judge against medalist ground truth on archived answers from five models across IPhO and IChO; two strong judges stay within one point of expert total scores. Medalist notes show that failures often stem from visual grounding, structure fidelity, and global problem control rather than missing terminology. Evaluating fourteen recent LLMs with interleaved solving, we find that top models obtain medal-equivalent rubric scores on several public international exams, while chemistry and long-horizon consistency remain key bottlenecks. We provide an interactive \href{https://science-arena.onrender.com/}{demo}.

</details>


### [176] [Learning-Assisted Congestion-Aware Route Scheduling for Semiconductor Fab Material Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30520)
*Hao Yin,Meiqi Tu,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于运输网络感知的动态拥堵表示（TN-DCR），在在线不泄露未来信息的约束下预测队列与传输时延并估计极端拥堵概率，结合经验贝叶斯校正与风险约束调度规则，在闭环实验中显著降低时延与等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体厂自动搬运系统需在线为每条运输命令调度接力路线；由于队列引起的尾部成本支配路线代价，且接力设备异构且部分可观测，路由选择需在决策时估计交付时间及拥堵风险以避免极端延迟。

Method: 基于历史观测构建静态有向运输图，设计Transport-Network-aware Dynamic Congestion Representation（TN-DCR），结合结构化路径先验、多窗口网络拥堵上下文、路径瓶颈暴露与图感知路径嵌入；在严格的预测时间不泄露信息约束下，分别训练队列时间与传输时间回归器，并训练序数多标签分类器输出校准的多阈值超额概率；用经验贝叶斯残差修正系统性低估；将预测作为成本嵌入轻量级运筹决策模型，求解在极端拥堵概率上界约束下的最短预计交付时间路线。

Result: 闭环受控评估显示：平均交付时间下降16.4%，内部资源等待时间下降22.6%，吞吐量基本不变；表明在风险约束下的TN-DCR能有效改善时延指标并降低尾部拥堵事件。

Conclusion: 提出的TN-DCR在半导体厂物料搬运调度场景中有效减少平均配送时间和内部等待时间，同时在吞吐量不变的前提下控制极端拥堵风险，验证了将网络感知的拥堵表示与风险约束调度规则结合的可行性和效果。

Abstract: Automated material handling systems in semiconductor fabs are operated by a material control system (MCS) that must schedule a relay route for every transport command online, before execution. This is a data-driven scheduling problem in which route cost is dominated in the upper tail by queueing at heterogeneous, partially observable relay equipment, so route selection requires estimating both delivery time and congestion risk at the decision moment. This paper proposes a transport-network-aware dynamic congestion representation (TN-DCR). Built on a static directed transport graph induced by historically observed relay segments, TN-DCR combines structural route priors, multi-window network-wide congestion context, route-level bottleneck exposure, and an inductive graph-aware route embedding, all constructed under a prediction-time-safety invariant that admits only information observed strictly before the prediction moment. The representation feeds separate queue- and transfer-time regressors and an ordinal multi-label classifier producing calibrated multi-threshold exceedance scores, with an empirical-Bayes stock-key residual correction reducing systematic queue-time underprediction. The predictions serve as costs in a risk-constrained route-scheduling rule that minimizes predicted delivery time subject to a bound on extreme-congestion probability, embedding the learned predictors within a lightweight operations-research decision model. In a controlled closed-loop evaluation, mean delivery time falls by 16.4\% and internal resource waiting time by 22.6\% while throughput remains essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [177] [DiffPDE: Masked Diffusion Language Models as PDE Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30532)
*Wenxuan Guo,Yuyang Hong,Lubin Fan,Zhaojin Fu,Lin Chen,Kun Ding,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对PDE代码修复的稀疏、局部错误问题，本文用离散扩散模型与局部重掩码/插补并辅以基于中间奖励的迭代强化学习，实现更高效且有竞争力的自动修复，比同规模自回归解码更快、更节省冗余生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE求解器代码修复常用自回归（左到右）解码，面对高度局部且稀疏的错误时存在大量冗余生成与计算浪费，且难以自然支持需要多步干预的耦合错误。

Method: 使用离散扩散语言模型作为基础生成器，引入局部重掩码（localized re-masking）与插补（infilling）策略只重生成疑似错误片段以保留正确上下文；针对耦合错误设计Iterative Debugging GRPO (ID-GRPO)，通过在单一轨迹中给予中间奖励来实现多轮逐步调试与修复。实验在PDEBench上与同规模自回归模型对比，评估准确率与修复速度。

Result: 在PDEBench上，DiffPDE取得与或优于同规模自回归模型的修复准确率，同时大幅减少需要重生成的tokens与修复时间；ID-GRPO使模型能在单次轨迹中完成多轮修正，提升对耦合错误的处理能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的DiffPDE通过离散扩散语言模型结合局部重掩码与填充策略，有效只重生成出错区域，并配合ID-GRPO强化学习实现多轮修复，最终在PDEBench上获得与同规模自回归模型可比或更优的修复准确率，同时显著加速修复过程。

Abstract: Existing approaches for synthesizing Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers predominantly rely on autoregressive models, yet their global left-to-right decoding incurs substantial redundancy when addressing inherently localized bugs. In this work, we challenge this inefficient paradigm and propose DiffPDE, a framework leveraging discrete diffusion language models for targeted code repair. By introducing a localized re-masking and infilling strategy, DiffPDE regenerates only erroneous regions while preserving correct context, naturally aligning generation with the sparse nature of PDE errors. Furthermore, to handle coupled bugs requiring sequential interventions, we present Iterative Debugging GRPO (ID-GRPO), a reinforcement learning scheme that enables multi-round debugging within single trajectories via intermediate rewards. Experiments on PDEBench show that DiffPDE achieves competitive accuracy, outperforms same-scale AR models, and significantly accelerates repair.

</details>


### [178] [GarmentWeaver: Schema-Aware Structured Synthesis for Multimodal Sewing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30550)
*Yinwen Lu,Weihao Luo,Yueqi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过模式感知的分层目标与可行性正则化，GarmentWeaver在多模态缝制纸样生成上实现了更准确和更可模拟的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将服装规范建模为单一长序列，导致结构与参数纠缠、冗余组件、局部细节不准确以及差的仿真兼容性，需一种结构感知的生成方式。

Method: 提出schema-aware的目标构建策略以激活与服装结构相关的分支，生成器基于预训练视觉-语言模型进行多模态理解，且引入feasibility-aware正则化以鼓励结构上有效和可模拟的输出。

Result: 大量实验表明GarmentWeaver比强基线生成更准确、更可执行的纸样，并带来更好的仿真结果，验证了schema-aware结构化生成的有效性。

Conclusion: GarmentWeaver通过模式感知的分层目标构建和基于预训练视觉-语言模型的生成器，有效提升了多模态缝制纸样生成的准确性与可执行性，并在可模拟性上优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Sewing pattern generation aims to infer executable sewing patterns from design cues such as sketches and textual descriptions. As an interpretable and simulation-compatible representation, sewing patterns are particularly valuable for digital garment creation. However, existing methods often model garment specifications as flat long sequences, which entangles garment structure with detailed parameters and leads to redundant components, inaccurate local details, and poor simulation compatibility. In this paper, we present GarmentWeaver, a schema-aware framework for multimodal Sewing pattern generation. GarmentWeaver constructs compact hierarchical targets by activating garment-relevant structural branches and predicts executable Sewing patterns in a structured manner. Specifically, we introduce a schema-aware target construction strategy, build the generator on top of a pretrained vision-language model for multimodal garment understanding, and impose feasibility-aware regularization to encourage structurally valid and simulation-compatible outputs. Extensive experiments show that GarmentWeaver produces more accurate and more executable sewing patterns than strong baselines, while also yielding better simulation results. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of schema-aware structured generation for reliable multimodal Sewing pattern prediction.

</details>


### [179] [AdaPath: Query-Adaptive Path-Finding via Path-Bank for Multi-Hop Implicit Biomedical KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30556)
*Jun Hyeong Kim,Dongki Kim,Yinhua Piao,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Path‑Bank的查询自适应元路径检索框架AdaPath，有效为生物医学多跳KGQA补充推理线索并剪枝稠密邻域，显著提升问答性能并发布分层基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学KGQA面临两大挑战：一是问题往往不显式给出中间推理步骤且可能有多条有效路径，二是生物医学KG连接稠密，常导致路径搜索误入歧途。通用领域方法未针对这两点设计。

Method: 构建包含查询语义与KG结构信息的Path‑Bank；针对每个问题检索适配的元路径（query‑adaptive meta‑paths）；以元路径为先验引导在生物医学KG上进行路径搜索与剪枝，结合下游QA模型评估路径质量。

Result: 在多项生物医学KGQA基准上，AdaPath优于各类基线方法；并发布BioStrat‑QA基准以按“暴露中间推理信息量”对多跳问题分层，验证了AdaPath在低暴露情形下维持有效路径发现的能力；代码已开源。

Conclusion: AdaPath通过从Path‑Bank检索查询自适应的元路径，提供缺失的查询线索并在稠密生物医学知识图谱中有效剪枝，从而实现稳健的多跳路径搜索，尤其在查询暴露较少中间推理信息时仍优于基线方法。

Abstract: Path-finding over knowledge graphs has become an effective way to ground LLM reasoning on multi-hop questions. However, biomedical QA introduces two distinct challenges that general-domain methods are not designed for: (i) queries do not expose intermediate reasoning and can be answered through multiple valid pathways, and (ii) biomedical knowledge graphs are densely connected, so path-finding methods easily take wrong turns. To address these challenges, we propose AdaPath, a path-finding framework that retrieves query-adaptive meta-paths from Path-Bank, which captures both query semantics and biomedical knowledge graph structure. AdaPath provides the missing cues in biomedical queries while effectively pruning dense knowledge graph neighborhoods during multi-hop reasoning. We further release BioStrat-QA, a biomedical KGQA benchmark that stratifies multi-hop queries by how much intermediate reasoning they expose. Across biomedical KGQA benchmarks, AdaPath consistently outperforms baselines, sustaining meaningful path-finding even when multi-hop queries expose less surface information. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jun-Hyeong-Kim/AdaPath.

</details>


### [180] [TuringLLM: Efficiently Scaling Foundation Models Toward Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30567)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yizhao Wang,Da Zhu,Hua Zhou,Yue He,Jiahui Hu,Shaman Tang,Hanlin Chen,Yuhua Wei,Anhua Liu,Shuang Su,Rui Xin,MingYuan Wang,MingHao Li,HaoJie Yang,Siqi Liu,Jianlei Zheng,WeiChao Huang,Qiman Wu,Hang Zhang,HongGou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Turing-20B-A2B通过动态Quantile Routing与混合注意力，在20B MoE框架下以约2B激活参数实现了能力与超长上下文效率的折中，优于Qwen3-8B Base并接近Qwen3.5-9B Base。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向需要长上下文和低预填延迟的物理AI应用，设计一个在保持模型能力的同时，控制单token激活参数规模与推理延迟的高效MoE模型。

Method: 采用Mixture-of-Experts架构（20B总参，约2B参数/token激活），引入Quantile Routing的动态top-k路由以实现按token分配专家并保持负载均衡与受控平均计算预算；部署时使用capacity-constrained路由对prompt prefill进行约束以提高执行规律性，预训练阶段保留无丢失（dropless）路由；混合注意力结构结合Lightning Attention与少数全注意层用于长上下文建模；三阶段渐进式训练课程，继续训练扩展至本地上下文长度128K，推理时通过YaRN扩展至512K。

Result: 在基线模型阶段（base-model stage），在整体通用能力上超过Qwen3-8B Base，接近Qwen3.5-9B Base，同时展现强长上下文能力与良好的预填延迟缩放；支持本地128K上下文，推理可扩展至512K。

Conclusion: Turing-20B-A2B 在激活约2B参数的“压缩活跃参数”设计下，在能力、长上下文可扩展性与实用推理效率之间取得了有效平衡，展现出优于 Qwen3-8B Base 且接近 Qwen3.5-9B Base 的整体能力，并保持强的长上下文性能与预填充延迟缩放特性。

Abstract: We present Turing-20B-A2B, a 20B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts language model that activates approximately 2B parameters per token, designed for long-context and latency-sensitive physical AI applications. The model adopts Quantile Routing in a dynamic top-k configuration, enabling token-adaptive expert allocation while maintaining balanced expert utilization and a controlled average compute budget. During deployment, we further apply capacity-constrained routing to prompt prefill for more regular and efficient expert execution, while retaining dropless routing during pretraining. Turing-20B-A2B also employs a hybrid attention architecture that combines Lightning Attention with a small number of full-attention layers for efficient long-context modeling. The model is pretrained with a progressive three-stage curriculum and extended to a native context length of 128K through continued pretraining, with further inference-time extension to 512K using YaRN. Despite its compact active-parameter budget, Turing-20B-A2B achieves, at the base-model stage, overall general capability exceeding Qwen3-8B Base and approaching Qwen3.5-9B Base, while maintaining strong long-context performance and favorable prefill-latency scaling. These results demonstrate an effective balance among model capability, long-context scalability, and practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [181] [Automated Testing of LLM-Based Post Hoc Explainers Using Model Checking as an Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30581)
*Dennis Gross,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用概率模型检测作为oracle并结合查询分类+难度优先级，提出了一套可自动化、系统化的LLM策略解释测试方法，并在多环境中证明其能区分模型性能并发现更难的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM被用作后验解释器，但经常生成看起来合理但不正确的解释；缺乏用于验证解释正确性的oracle，且自然语言查询缺乏结构，阻碍系统化测试与自动评估。

Method: 利用概率模型检测计算环境级别的精确参考结果作为判定oracle；构造基于环境事实的查询分类学以结构化自然语言查询空间；为每类查询定义诊断难度分数并据此对测试用例进行优先级排序；在七个MDP环境上对三种不同规模的开源模型进行评估。

Result: 在七个MDP环境下，基于oracle的测试区分了三种模型：推理型模型通过率85%，中等规模模型70%，1B参数模型低于随机基线；优先级排序能显著发现比随机采样更困难的用例；该方法为无模型环境中的LLM解释可信度提供参考。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种基于概率模型检测的测试框架，用以为LLM生成的序贯决策策略解释提供可自动判定的oracle，并结合查询分类法与问题难度优先级生成系统化测试用例，从而衡量解释的忠实性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used as post hoc explainers of sequential decision-making policies, producing natural-language explanations of why an action was chosen. However, LLMs often generate plausible but incorrect statements, and no existing approach systematically tests whether such explanations are faithful to the underlying environment. Two classic software testing challenges stand in the way: there is no oracle for the correctness of an explanation, and the test inputs, natural language queries about a policy's behavior, lack the structure needed for systematic test case generation. We address both. Probabilistic model checking provides the test oracle, computing exact reference results against which LLM answers are graded automatically. A taxonomy of post hoc query categories structures the input space around the environment-level facts from which policy explanations are composed; test cases generated from it are prioritized by question-specific diagnostic difficulty scores. Across seven MDP environments, the testing separates three open-weight LLMs: a reasoning model passes 85% of test cases, a mid-size model 70%, and a 1B model falls below the random baseline, while prioritization surfaces significantly harder cases than random selection. Our results indicate how trustworthy LLM-generated explanations are in model-free settings, where the same LLMs are used but no oracle exists to verify them.

</details>


### [182] [Geometry of Divergence: Tracking Hidden-State Trajectories for Adaptive Multi-Turn Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30650)
*Jie Liang,Zhengxin Yu,Hamid Nasiri,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用隐藏态轨迹的几何特征（曲率与方差斜率）可早期检测多轮推理失败并指导轻量干预，从而在多任务多模型设置下显著提高成功率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决 LLM agent 在长期、多轮交互中因上下文累积导致的内部表征漂移问题——这种漂移会混淆构建性推理与无关表示变化，且在资源受限场景下需要早期、轻量的失败检测与纠正机制。

Method: 方法包括：将多轮交互视为隐藏态随回合演化的轨迹，定义并计算两个几何量——时间曲率（衡量方向一致性）与方差斜率（衡量探索空间收缩/扩张）；在四个任务、三种基础 LLM 上测量这些信号；将单个对话拆解为由四种动作（Read, Write, Respond, Transfer）组成的三动作链，按动作类型分析信号可分离性；基于轨迹几何识别关键回合并进行策略性早停或重推以提高成功率与降低 token 成本。

Result: 实验结果表明：时间曲率与方差斜率能在对话结束前显著区分正确/错误回合；信号的判别能力依赖于动作链模式；基于轨迹几何的干预使 τ-Bench 的任务成功率由 24.1% 提升至 39.6%，同时 token 成本下降约 11.2%。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：通过把多轮推理建模为基础 LLM 的隐藏态轨迹，并以时间曲率（temporal curvature）和方差斜率（variance slope）两类几何信号表征轨迹，可以在对话完成前区分正确与错误的推理过程，从而定位关键回合并基于此进行干预，提高任务成功率并节约资源。

Abstract: LLM agents need to sustain goal-consistent reasoning across long multi-turn interactions under strict resource constraints. However, as the multi-turn context accumulates, it can destabilize the underlying LLM's internal representation of task-relevant information from earlier turns, blurring the boundary between constructive reasoning and representation drift. We formulate multi-turn reasoning as a hidden-state trajectory of the underlying LLM that is characterized via two complementary signals: temporal curvature that captures the directional consistency of turn-to-turn updates, and variance slope which measures the expansion or contraction of the exploration space. Across four tasks and three underlying LLMs, we observed that these geometric signals distinguish between correct and incorrect episodes prior to completion. We further decompose each episode into three-action chains formed from four actions (Read, Write, Respond, Transfer) and show that separability is action-dependent, with different signals distinguishing various chain patterns. Our experiments demonstrate that trajectory geometry can identify critical turns in the reasoning process, increasing task success rates on $τ$-Bench from 24.1% to 39.6% while reducing token cost by 11.2%.

</details>


### [183] [PyKEEN-NSX: A Modular Framework for Static, Dynamic and Schema-Aware Negative Sampling in PyKEEN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30652)
*Ivan Diliso,Nicola Fanizzi,Claudia d'Amato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PyKEEN-NSX：一个把候选池生成与选择策略分离的模块化负采样扩展，兼容PyKEEN并实现六种采样器；实验揭示受约束池经常不足，导致依赖随机回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图嵌入库普遍只支持简单负采样策略，缺乏统一框架来开发更高级或自定义的负采样器，而负采样策略对KGE训练效果重要且设计实现具有挑战。

Method: 设计一个工程化的模块化抽象：将Pool生成（context-conditioned）与Selector分离；在该框架中实现六种负采样器；保持与PyKEEN接口兼容；在四个数据集上评估候选池可用性并分析回退发生情况。

Result: 提供了一个可扩展的负采样框架（PyKEEN-NSX）并实现多种采样器，证明其与PyKEEN兼容；实验发现约束性池在实际数据集上经常不足，提示很多策略的期望效果会被随机补充所替代。

Conclusion: 提出并实现了PyKEEN-NSX，一个在PyKEEN之上提供模块化负采样抽象的扩展。该架构将候选负样本池的生成（带显式上下文）与负样本选择策略分离，支持静态、基于模式和动态的方法，并实现了六种负采样器，兼容现有PyKEEN工作流。实证分析显示，在四个数据集上，受约束的候选池常常小于请求的负样本数，从而大量依赖随机回退策略。

Abstract: Embedding methods have become popular due to their scalability on link prediction and/or triple classification tasks on Knowledge Graphs (KGs). Embedding models are trained relying on both positive and negative samples of triples. However, since KGs generally contain only positive assertions, negative samples are artificially generated through negative sampling strategies, ranging from simple random corruption to more sophisticated approaches that exploit structural, semantic, or embedding information. The design and implementation of advanced negative samplers remains challenging, as most popular Knowledge Graph Embedding (KGE) libraries provide support only for basic strategies and lack a unified framework for developing more advanced and customized solutions. To address this gap, we introduce PyKEEN-NSX, an extension of PyKEEN, the popular KGE framework, that provides a modular engineered abstraction for negative sampling. The proposed architecture separates the generation of candidate negative pools, conditioned on an explicit context, from the selection strategy, enabling the development and integration of static, schema-aware and dynamic approaches within a consistent framework. Based on this abstraction, we implement six negative samplers, while remaining fully compatible with existing PyKEEN workflows and pipelines. As a proof of concept, we study negative availability across four datasets, showing that constrained pools frequently fall below the requested number of negatives, so that the encoded criterion is to a large extent replaced by the random fallback that supplements them.

</details>


### [184] [HiRS-Agent: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Reliable Long-Horizon Remote Sensing Task Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30672)
*Boyang Mu,Zhiwei Wei,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiRS-Agent以Manager—Specialist两层多智能体架构和监督+验证驱动的分层强化学习，在长时程遥感任务中显著提高了工具使用稳定性和任务正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一决策框架难以处理遥感任务的多阶段、相互依赖特性，导致执行不稳定、工具误用及错误在阶段间传播，因而需引入结构化、多主体的协同机制来提升可靠性。

Method: 构建两层协同架构：Manager层负责动态路由、步级验证、重规划与终止控制；Specialist层按照遥感工作流组织领域专用工具，负责子任务推理与工具执行。训练采用两阶段监督微调策略，随后引入基于验证的分层强化学习以联合优化协调策略与工具使用策略。

Result: 在Earth-Agent Benchmark与ThinkGeo数据集上的实验表明，HiRS-Agent显著提升了长时程工具使用能力与最终任务正确率，实验结果支持分层多智能体协作对遥感代理系统的改进。

Conclusion: 提出的HiRS-Agent通过分层多智能体结构，有效提升了面向长时程遥感任务的工具使用稳定性和最终任务正确性，验证了结构化协同在遥感智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and multimodal models have pushed remote sensing (RS) processing from simple perception models to agentic systems designed to tackle complex, long-horizon RS tasks. However, existing systems often rely on monolithic decision-making frameworks, which fail to accommodate the multi-stage, interdependent nature of RS tasks. This centralized approach leads to challenges such as unstable task execution, incorrect tool usage, and error propagation across stages. To address these issues, we propose HiRS-Agent, a hierarchical multi-agent system for long-horizon RS task solving. HiRS-Agent adopts a two-level collaborative architecture: the Manager Layer handles dynamic routing, step-level verification, replanning, and termination control, while the Specialist Layer organizes domain-specific tools according to the RS workflow and is responsible for subtask reasoning and tool execution. To further enhance the system's capability, we introduce a two-stage supervised tuning strategy and a verification-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning stage to jointly optimize coordination and tool-use policies. Experiments on Earth-Agent Benchmark and ThinkGeo show that HiRS-Agent substantially improves long-horizon tool-use capability and final-task correctness, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured multi-agent collaboration for reliable RS agents. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/IntelliSensing/HiRS-Agent.

</details>


### [185] [MedAgent-R1: Faithfulness-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30676)
*Jiangwang Chen,Chenghao Zhang,Hengxing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对抗RL检索代理中的“自信幻觉”，通过将准确性奖励与证据落地硬门控结合并加入检索质量信号，可大幅降低引用捏造并提升理由的可验证性，形成更安全可信的医疗推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI的错误不仅是错答案，更危险的是用看似基于证据的虚构推理去误导临床决策，因此需要代理不仅给出正确结论，还必须给出可与引用证据核验的忠实理由。

Method: 先识别RL训练的检索代理在仅以结果奖励时的系统性失败模式；度量引用捏造率、证据完整性与准确性等指标；提出“faithfulness-gated reward”设计——把准确性奖励通过一个“证据落地”的硬门控才能放行，并辅以检索有效性与回答简洁性信号以封堵利用路径；基于该奖励训练出MedAgent-R1，并与监督基线和GPT-4o在多项信度与安全指标上比较。

Result: 在相同的代理检索设置下，采用faithfulness-gated reward将引用捏造率从31.8%降到4.7%，证据完整性从58.7提升到82.6，保持75.1%的准确率；在HealthBench Safety上提升13.2个百分点；在事实支持与过度宣称专门维度上超过GPT-4o（Factual Support 4.55 vs. 4.25；Overclaiming 4.40 vs. 4.15），但总体准确性仍低于GPT-4o。

Conclusion: 在基于检索的医疗推理代理中，仅以任务结果为奖励会催生“自信幻觉”（confident hallucination）；通过将正确性奖励与证据落地的硬门控相结合，并加入检索有效性与简洁性信号，可显著提升理由的可验证性并降低引用捏造，形成更可信的医疗推理代理。

Abstract: When medical AI systems hallucinate clinical reasoning, the consequences extend beyond incorrect answers: fabricated justifications that superficially reference retrieved evidence can mislead clinicians into unsafe treatment decisions. Medical reasoning agents must therefore produce not only correct answers but also faithful justifications that clinicians can verify against cited evidence. We identify a systematic failure mode in RL-trained retrieval agents: outcome-only rewards improve accuracy while degrading faithfulness, a phenomenon we term confident hallucination. The agent learns to answer from parametric memory and backfill plausible but unsupported justifications; citation fabrication rates rise from 16.5% to 31.8% even as accuracy improves by 5 points over the supervised baseline. We address this with a faithfulness-gated reward design: accuracy credit is conditioned on evidence grounding via a hard gate, complemented by retrieval validity and conciseness signals that close exploitation paths unique to agentic retrieval. The resulting system, MedAgent-R1, reduces citation fabrication from 31.8% to 4.7% and raises evidence completeness from 58.7 to 82.6 while maintaining 75.1% accuracy, with 13.2-point gains on HealthBench Safety. Under the same agentic retrieval setup, MedAgent-R1 outscores GPT-4o on faithfulness-specific dimensions (Factual Support 4.55 vs. 4.25; Overclaiming 4.40 vs. 4.15) while remaining below GPT-4o in overall accuracy, suggesting that explicit faithfulness training yields evidence-grounding gains not achieved by scaling alone.

</details>


### [186] [ATLAS: Dual-Horizon Diagnostic Evaluation for Industrial Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30685)
*Wei Chen,Peilun Zhou,Zhaoyu Hu,Jiajun Chai,Zhongni Hou,Yufei Zhang,Derong Xu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATLAS 用双视角可执行诊断信号和经校准/蒸馏的判定器，解决工具调用型 LLM 代理在定位缺陷与保持长期服务一致性方面的评估与策略优化问题，并在工业生产流量中取得实际提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业化部署的工具调用型 LLM 代理在动态业务场景中需迭代使用工具，单一的最终结果评估掩盖了缺陷产生的位置和随时间的服务一致性问题，因而需要能既定位执行缺陷又评估持续交互表现的评价框架。

Method: 提出“请求视界”（trajectory-wise）与“交互视界”（user-wise）两类诊断信号，定义明确的证据范围与决策边界；用业务日志中的高置信参考校准 LLM 判定界面；在需要时将 LLM 判定行为蒸馏为低延迟/低成本的诊断模型；把所得反馈用于策略（policy）优化，并通过回放实验与线上 A/B 验证效果。

Result: 在美团小团生产流量上的评估显示：诊断信号与高置信参考具有较好保真度；回放实验表明基于诊断反馈可改进策略；线上 A/B 显示用户参与度、下游业务指标和抽样人工审核质量均有提升。

Conclusion: ATLAS 提供了一种可执行的双视角（请求轨迹与持续交互）诊断评估框架，能够定位工具调用型大语言模型代理的缺陷来源并评估长期服务一致性。通过将诊断信号具体化、校准 LLM 判定器并蒸馏为低成本模型，ATLAS 在美团小团生产流量上实现了离线验证与在线 A/B 提升，证明其在诊断与策略优化上有效。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in user-facing services that require iterative tool use under dynamic business conditions. Reliable evaluation is essential for sustained improvement: it must reveal capability deficiencies, inform priorities, and assess interventions. Yet industrial agent service unfolds both through the iterative trajectory of a current request and through continued user interaction. Final-outcome assessment can therefore obscure where deficiencies arise and whether later service remains aligned with context from earlier exchanges. We propose ATLAS, a dual-horizon diagnostic evaluation framework for industrial tool-use agents. At the request horizon, trajectory-wise diagnostic signals relate deficiencies to execution locations and capability concerns. At the interaction horizon, user-wise signals assess whether service remains responsive across continued interaction. Together, these views provide structured diagnostic evidence for analyzing execution deficiencies and sustained service behavior. ATLAS instantiates them as executable signals with explicit evidence scopes and decision boundaries. LLM judge interfaces are calibrated against high-confidence references from real business logs; when needed, their decision behavior is distilled into efficient diagnostic models for lower-latency, lower-cost evaluation. The resulting feedback supports policy optimization. We evaluate ATLAS on Meituan Xiaotuan production traffic. Offline experiments assess diagnostic-signal fidelity and replay-based policy improvement, while online A/B experiments show concurrent gains in user engagement, downstream business outcomes, and sampled human-audit quality.

</details>


### [187] [Multimodal Adaptive Expert Selection with Text Routing and Ordinal Prototype Optimization for Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30726)
*Xiaode Chen,Jiakang Yu,Hongtao Deng,Huina Qu,Xun Zhu,Yinxia Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAESTRO通过文本驱动的动态MoE路由与序数感知原型对比学习，改进多模态情感表征并在常用基准上达到SOTA，同时增强了模型决策的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前M SA方法受限于静态计算图对所有样本一视同仁导致表示欠适应，以及通用对比学习目标忽视情感强度的序数关系。

Method: 提出Text-Guided Hybrid Mixture-of-Experts（基于语言上下文进行路由以动态激活音视频专家）与Ordinal-aware Prototype Contrastive Learning（在原型学习中加入基于距离的惩罚以保留情感序数），联合训练以优化融合表征。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI基准上取得SOTA结果；定性分析显示路由策略具有可解释性，且O-PCL使潜在空间呈现有序结构。

Conclusion: MAESTRO通过文本引导的混合专家选择模块与序数感知原型对比学习，能够动态调配音视频专家并在潜在空间中保持情感强度的序数结构，从而在CMU-MOSI/CMU-MOSEI上实现了最先进的性能并增强了可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) is a fundamental component of affective computing that aims to decipher complex emotional states by integrating verbal content with non-verbal cues including vocal intonation and facial micro-expressions. While recent disentanglement-based approaches have advanced the field, their potential is hindered by two methodological challenges. First, static computation graphs process all samples indiscriminately regardless of semantic complexity, which leads to suboptimal representation for diverse emotional expressions and contextual scenarios. Second, generic contrastive objectives often neglect the intrinsic ordinal hierarchy of sentiment intensities. To systematically address these limitations, we introduce Multimodal Adaptive Expert Selection with Text Routing and Ordinal prototype optimization (MAESTRO), a novel framework designed to dynamically orchestrate and refine multimodal representations. Drawing inspiration from an orchestra conductor, we design a Text-Guided Hybrid Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) mechanism. Unlike static fusion, this module utilizes linguistic context as a routing signal to dynamically activate specific audio-visual experts, thereby resolving cross-modal ambiguity through adaptive feature enhancement. Furthermore, to capture fine-grained sentiment gradations, we propose an Ordinal-aware Prototype Contrastive Learning (O-PCL). By incorporating distance-based penalties into the prototype learning objective, O-PCL enforces a structured latent space that preserves the natural order of emotion. Extensive experiments on the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI benchmarks demonstrate that MAESTRO achieves state-of-the-art performance, and qualitative analysis further confirms the interpretability of our dynamic routing paradigm.

</details>


### [188] [Autoregressive Mosaics: Probing 2D Spatial Reasoning in Text-Only Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30751)
*Ashwin Nedungadi,Stefan Oehmcke,Stefan Lüdtke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AM-Bench把翻译能力和空间构图能力分离开来：文本+代码模型普遍能把已指定几何翻译成代码，但开放式布局能力差异大；输出媒介（如SVG）能显著改善表现，内部存在粗略前置计划但模型在生成过程中动态维护几何状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分模型生成可识别图像时是否有内在的2D空间表征，还是仅仅把文字描述翻译成代码；明确模型、任务和输出介质在空间表现中的作用。

Method: 提出Autoregressive Mosaics (AM-Bench)基准，设置两类任务：Translation（给出完全指定的几何描述，要求生成产生该图像的代码）和Layout（给出欠定提示，要求模型构图）。在八个开放权重的文本+代码模型上评估；进行输出介质消融（代码 vs 原始SVG）；并通过探测模型激活分布来分析内部表征与生成过程。

Result: 所有模型在Translation任务上都表现良好，但在Layout任务上差异显著，表明开放式构图能力与代码生成能力解耦。将输出从程序化代码替换为原始SVG普遍提升了布局成绩。激活探测显示：生成前存在粗略布局计划，但仅反映提示中的布局信息；生成时模型并非执行固定计划，而是跟踪生成过程中不断演变的几何状态。

Conclusion: 文本和代码训练的自回归大模型在生成可识别图像时，并非仅靠代码生成能力。2D空间表现同时依赖模型本身和输出表达介质，且不完全源自内部静态的空间计划。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained only on text and code can sometimes generate programs that draw recognizable images. However, it is unclear whether this reflects an internal representation of 2D spatial layout or simply the ability to translate spatial descriptions into code. We introduce Autoregressive Mosaics (AM-Bench), a benchmark that separates these factors: First, a translation task gives a model a fully specified geometry of a picture in words as a prompt and asks for the code that produces it. Second, a layout task requires the model to compose an image from an underspecified prompt. Across eight open-weight text-and-code-only models, all models reliably translate specified geometry into code, but their open-ended layout performance differs substantially, indicating that these differences are not explained by code-generation ability alone. An output-medium ablation further shows that the interface or medium of expression that the model uses matters: replacing procedural code with raw SVG improves layout scores across all models. Finally, probing model activations shows that a coarse layout plan is present before generation, but reflects only the layout implied by the prompt. During generation, models track the evolving geometric state instead of executing an initially fixed plan. Overall, these results show that 2D spatial performance in text-only LLMs depends on both the model and the output medium, and is not explained by code-generation ability alone.

</details>


### [189] [Which Rules Matter Now? Policy-Centroid Routing Before an Intelligent System Acts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30757)
*Thomson D. Nguy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“政策质心路由”作为在裁决前的注意力层，通过语义压缩和相似度阈值筛出需审查的政策体制；设计了严格可检验的研究蓝图，但尚无实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能系统在裁决动作是否合法或允许前，需要先注意到哪些政策体制被触及；天然语言策略、动作描述不完整且可能触发多重体制，首要问题是注意力而非判断。

Method: 将每个政策体制的自然语言规范压缩为一个或多个语义代表（centroid），将拟议动作映射到同一语义空间，按声明的度量计算相似度，并把超过阈值的政策体制路由到人工或权威审查，产出审查议程而非最终判定。

Result: 提出了六个可证伪命题和七项后续比较研究设计（与结构化工作流、词汇/语义检索、层级/直接分类及有选择预测对比），包含合成示例，但未报告实证有效性结果。

Conclusion: 方法对规模化、多制度重叠情形具有潜在价值，关键风险在于压缩丢失稀有/重叠义务和阈值误设；需要公开基准、详尽构造细节与实证比较来验证其检出率、审查负担与弃权策略。

Abstract: Before an intelligent system can decide whether an action is allowed, it must first know which rules the action has approached. A single proposed action can implicate several policy regimes at once. Their requirements may stack, overlap, or qualify one another, yet many remain written in natural language while the action itself arrives as an incomplete description of intent. The first problem is not judgment. It is attention.
  Policy-centroid routing creates a layer before adjudication. It compresses expressions within each policy regime into one or more representative centroids, places the proposed action in the same semantic space, applies a declared measure, and routes every regime crossing a declared threshold to authoritative review. Several regimes may trigger at once. The output is a review agenda, not permission, prohibition, legality, breach, compliance, certification, or enforcement.
  The paper develops six falsifiable propositions and seven follow-on studies comparing the hypothesis with structured workflows, lexical and semantic retrieval, hierarchical and direct classification, and selective prediction under matched review burden. The studies are designed to identify where policy geometry recovers applicable regimes, where compression loses rare or overlapping obligations, and where the mechanism should abstain. The paper includes a synthetic worked example and reports no empirical efficacy result.

</details>


### [190] [SkillZip Pro: Execution-Aware Dynamic Compression of Progressively Loaded Skills for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30785)
*Xiaofan Bai,Chao Liu,Hongqiang Lin,Di Wu,Mingli Song,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向逐步加载技能捆绑的无评估跨文件压缩器：在保证路由与入口可达性的前提下，按策略重写目录以减小部署与运行 token 成本，提供 One-Shot/Continual 与 Persistent/Transient 模式，并在生产评测中显著降低 token 开销且无质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级 agent 技能是目录式捆绑、并非孤立提示；根加载与逐步加载导致压缩仅针对根时无法覆盖大多数部署成本，或将分支细节搬到始终加载的上下文；而平坦化会破坏逐步加载边界。需要一种既能减小部署与运行成本又不破坏逐步加载与路由的压缩方法。

Method: 跨文件压缩：当根或声明的环境合约已提供某些内容时，从引用或子技能中移除重复内容；保留路由可达性，确保所有必需文件与入口在重写后仍可访问。支持配置轴：One-Shot（重建全 bundle）与 Continual（重用状态、采用 Zip-on-Write）；Persistent（改写发货 bundle）与 Transient（保持字节恒等、构建任务视图以减小每次运行上下文）。条目合约用于标注私有/公共/条件资源，且多入口审计保证公共子技能的独立性。

Result: 在一个生产内容审核技能与工业级多轮 harness 测试中，\method 在无质量损失下移除 38% 的 skill bundle tokens 与 10.4% 的端到端每次运行 tokens；未经保护的 71% 配置则在单侧假阳性上最多损失 26 个准确率点。对多入口 bundle，\method 有效减少 token 成本并近乎完美保留每条路由与公共入口。

Conclusion: \method 是一个无评估（evaluation-free）的压缩器，面向完整的逐步加载（progressively loaded）技能目录捆绑，能够在不改动 agent harness 的前提下输出常规目录并减少部署与运行时的 token 成本，同时保留每条可达路由与可直接调用的入口。

Abstract: Production agent skills are directory bundles, not isolated prompts. The root is loaded at activation; references, schemas, scripts, assets, and nested subskills are loaded only when an execution path needs them. Compressing only the root misses most deployment cost and may move branch-specific details into the always-loaded context. Flattening instead destroys progressive-loading boundaries.
  We introduce \method, an evaluation-free compressor for complete, progressively loaded skill bundles. It leaves the agent harness unchanged and emits an ordinary directory. The method combines two safeguards. First, it compresses \emph{across files}, removing content from a reference or subskill when the root or a declared environment contract already provides it. Second, it preserves routing, so every required file and directly callable entry remains reachable after rewriting. Users can configure \method along two independent axes. \emph{One-Shot} mode rebuilds the full bundle; \emph{Continual} mode reuses state and applies Zip-on-Write after each evolution patch. \emph{Persistent} compression rewrites the shipped bundle to reduce storage and runtime context. \emph{Transient} compression keeps that bundle byte-identical and builds a task-specific view, reducing only per-run context after build cost. Entry contracts mark private, public, and conditional resources; a multi-entry audit preserves standalone public subskills.
  On a production content-moderation skill evaluated by our industrial multi-round harness, \method removes \hl{38\%} of skill bundle tokens and \hl{10.4\%} of end-to-end per-run tokens with no quality loss, while an unprotected 71\% configuration loses up to 26 accuracy points to one-sided false positives. On a multi-entry bundle, \method effeciently reduces token cost while near-perfectly preserving every route and public entry.

</details>


### [191] [HSRM: Hidden-State Reward Models for Test-Time Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30841)
*Xianzhi Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HSRM用小型Transformer直接读生成器隐藏态来验证数学解，既省时又省参数，在绝大多数设置下优于大型文本验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的验证需要重新阅读每个候选解，推理时代价高；但LLM内部隐藏态常常包含关于答案正确性的信号，因此可探索直接利用隐藏态做轻量验证以节省计算并保持性能。

Method: 冻结原始生成器，在推理步骤边界提取隐藏态表示；用一个约2M参数的小型Transformer编码器作为打分/排序器；训练采用自生成轨迹并用结果（正确/错误）作为标签；推理时直接读取并输入隐藏态以对候选解评分，无需重新生成或重读文本。

Result: HSRM（Hidden-State Reward Model）通过直接读取生成器在推理步骤边界处的隐藏态来对候选数学解进行排序验证，而不是重新处理文本，从而实现了轻量且高效的验证模块。该模型基于冻结的生成器提取隐藏态，使用小型Transformer编码器对候选解进行排名，训练数据来自生成器自我生成的轨迹并包含结果标签，无需人工过程监督或大型预训练验证器。实验在四个数学推理基准上进行，在16种生成器–数据集组合中有15种匹配或优于一个55M参数的文本能量验证器，而HSRM仅用约2M参数。

Conclusion: 隐藏态中包含足以区分正确与错误解的信号；基于这些隐藏态训练的小型验证器能显著降低验证计算开销，同时保持甚至提高验证效果。HSRM为重复利用生成过程已有表示以实现高效验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models can often generate plausible mathematical reasoning traces, but reliably identifying the correct solution among multiple candidates remains a key challenge. Existing test-time reasoning pipelines typically rely on text-based verifiers that re-read each generated solution, making verification an expensive component of inference. Prior work has shown, however, that LLMs often encode correctness-related signals in their internal representations, including awareness of when their own answers are likely to be wrong. Building on this observation, we introduce HSRM, a lightweight hidden-state reward model that verifies candidate solutions by directly reading the generator's internal representations rather than re-processing its text. HSRM extracts hidden states from a frozen generator at reasoning-step boundaries and uses a small Transformer encoder to rank candidates. It is trained from self-generated trajectories with outcome labels, requiring neither human-written process supervision nor a large pretrained verifier. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, HSRM matches or outperforms a 55M-parameter text-only energy verifier in 15 of 16 generator--dataset settings while using only about 2M parameters, providing an efficient alternative to text-only verification by reusing representations already computed during generation.

</details>


### [192] [VFR-Audit: Verdict-Level Reliability for Fairness Audits in Hospital Length-of-Stay Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30846)
*Md Jannatul Rakib Joy,Viet Vo,Caslon Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFR-Audit用分层自助法计算Verdict Flip Rate，给出判决翻转概率并结合三条可靠性轴，弥补了把度量不确定性转为判决稳定性这一空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性方法（Bayesian后验、bootstrap置信区间、置换检验）仅在连续指标层面量化不确定性，但审计決策是二元的“通过/不通过”，将指标不确定性翻译为判决稳定性通常需手工、不可扩展。临床AI审计跨时间与跨站点重复发生，需一个可自动化、可比较的判决稳定性标量与多维可靠性度量，供医院治理、支付方与监管者据以行动。

Method: 对每个（模型, 度量, 属性）单元进行分层bootstrap重采样，在每次重采样上计算连续度量并与预设阈值比较以得出二元判决；VFR为重采样序列中判决与原始判决相反的频率（概率），被限定在[0,0.5]。在此基础上评估：1) 同队列重采样稳定性（VFR的置信分布）；2) 审计规模敏感性（对不同样本量重复计算VFR）；3) 跨医院一致性（对多个站点的判决用Fleiss’ κ度量一致性）。还可将偏差缓解措施（如重加权、分组阈值移动）并入流程，比较其对VFR与AUROC/AUPRC的影响。

Result: 提出并实现了VFR-Audit框架，核心量化指标为Verdict Flip Rate（VFR），通过分层自助重采样估计审计判决（通过/不通过）反转的概率，数值范围在0到0.5之间。框架同时报告三条可靠性轴：同队列重采样稳定性、审计样本量敏感性，以及基于Fleiss’ κ的跨医院判决一致性。VFR-Audit能把连续公平性指标的不确定性直接映射为判决稳定性，便利大规模（模型、度量、属性）格的自动化审计和偏差缓解措施的效用-稳定性权衡评估。

Conclusion: VFR-Audit为临床AI公平性审计提供了可操作的、可扩展的判决稳定性量化方法：决策者可据此判断通过/未通过是否具有稳健性、比较不同缓解策略在判决和模型判别性（AUROC/AUPRC）间的权衡，并在多站点、重复审计场景中自动化生成可靠性报告。

Abstract: Fairness audits in clinical Artificial Intelligence convert continuous fairness metrics into binary pass-or-fail verdicts against operational thresholds, where hospital governance boards, payers, and regulators act on the resulting verdicts. Such audits are repeated over time and across hospital sites, thus the same verdict can flip between pass and fail across audits. Existing uncertainty methods such as Bayesian posteriors, bootstrap confidence intervals, and permutation tests address verdict instability only at the continuous-metric level. Converting metric-level uncertainty into a verdict-stability claim remains a manual step that scales poorly across the (model, metric, attribute) cells an audit covers. Existing uncertainty methods also leave open whether bias-mitigation steps, such as reweighing or per-group threshold shifts, yield a stable passing verdict at the cost of model discrimination measured as AUROC or AUPRC.To address this verdict-stability gap, we propose VFR-Audit, a framework built around the Verdict Flip Rate (VFR), a scalar bounded between 0 and 0.5 that measures the probability of verdict reversal under stratified bootstrap resampling. VFR-Audit reports VFR alongside three reliability axes, namely within-cohort resampling stability, audit-size sensitivity, and cross-hospital verdict agreement via Fleiss' kappa.

</details>


### [193] [Predicting Residential Rents in Dakar Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30865)
*Amadou Tidiane Kassa Diallo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了1507条达喀尔租赁数据集，工程化若干特征（如奢侈度、关键词质量分），采用泄漏保护的KFold目标编码处理位置变量，比较线性回归、随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM并通过Optuna对树模型贝叶斯优化。最佳为XGBoost：R^2=0.847，MAE=210,902 XOF，RMSE=324,195 XOF。使用原生gain与SHAP评估特征重要性，发现位置在gain中权重低但在SHAP中为第二重要，提示目标编码在异质性解释上的方法学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达喀尔租赁市场文献稀缺但租赁占家庭比例高，需一个端到端、可解释的机器学习基准来量化租金决定因素并为政策/市场分析提供工具。

Method: 系统爬取并清洗1507条房源，加入四个自定义特征（含奢侈度与关键词质量分），对位置类别采用泄漏-free KFold目标编码；训练并比较五种模型（线性、RF、XGBoost、LightGBM），用Optuna对树模型做贝叶斯超参优化；用XGBoost原生gain与SHAP值评估变量重要性。

Result: 最佳模型为经优化的XGBoost（R^2=0.847，MAE≈210.9k XOF，RMSE≈324.2k XOF）。特征重要性分析显示原生gain与SHAP排序不一致，尤其是位置变量的影响在两者间差别显著，提示目标编码对解释性的影响。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个可解释的达喀尔租赁价格预测基准并指出：（1）高性能模型可达较高R^2；（2）目标编码的评估指标可能导致特征重要性排序偏差；（3）需引入地理信息与不确定性评估等改进方向。

Abstract: Dakar's residential rental market remains poorly documented despite its economic and social importance: 54.4% of households are renters, compared to 23.3% nationally. This study develops a complete machine learning pipeline to predict residential rents in Dakar, from data collection to model interpretation. An original dataset of 1,507 rental listings was built through systematic web scraping and a documented cleaning pipeline, then enriched with four purpose-built features, including a luxury score and a keyword-based quality score.
